<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为将熔炼、还原、雾化和淬火集成于一步，以氩氢等离子体反应器制备优质球形粉末并控制化学组成，需要对反应器进行多物理场建模、实时监测与数据驱动优化。

Method: 利用热电偶和压力传感器采集实际实验的每日数据；以COMSOL Multiphysics为核心求解器，建立反应腔与收敛-扩张喷嘴的多物理场数字孪生；用AI模型补充COMSOL，实现数据同化和优化；开发定制相变粒子追踪层并在Ti-6Al-4V上验证。

Result: 构建了反应腔和收敛-扩张喷嘴的COMSOL孪生模型，并在Ti64上验证了相变粒子追踪层；展示了COMSOL仿真与AI数字代理协同形成自洽优化循环的潜力。

Conclusion: COMSOL多物理场仿真与AI代理模型的协同可支撑反应器全自主实时控制和工艺优化，为集成式等离子体粉末制备提供数字孪生与优化框架。

Abstract: The principal proof of concept revolves around an argon-hydrogen plasma reactor that melts, reduces, atomizes and quenches critical raw material in one step, with premium spherical powder as the deliverable output and control of the composition chemistry. Each usage of the reactor is monitored through thermocouples and pressure sensors, which provide a daily data source of the real-world experiments. The reactor is modeled through COMSOL Multiphysics, which represents the core solver used to provide multiphysics simulations. The usage of COMSOL is complemented with Artificial Intelligence (AI) models, to enable seamless data assimilation and optimization. This paper presents the COMSOL twin of the reaction chamber and converging-diverging nozzle, together with a custom phase-change particle-tracing layer validated on Ti-6Al-4V (Ti64). Moreover, we highlight how the synergy between COMSOL simulations and AI-based digital surrogates can be leveraged to build self-consistent optimization loops geared toward (i) fully autonomous live control of the reactor and (ii) optimization of the process.

</details>


### [66] [Agentic Porting, Construction and Initial Verification and Validation of Libraries within the Open Source Unified TRAnsient Multi-Physics Advanced Reactor simulation Kit (Outram Park) Part II: Nuclear Data and Neutronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15258)
*Theodore Kay Chen,SiCong Xiao*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 将 NJOY 和 OpenMC 以 agentic Rust 方式移植到 Outram Park，形成从 ENDF 到蒙特卡罗模拟的便捷流水线；目前为原型，已演示 U-238 多普勒展宽截面并与 OpenMC HDF5 对比，并完成 ICSBEP 与 TRISO 燃料 FHR 球床的初步测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为 Outram Park 提供便捷的 ENDF 到 Monte Carlo 模拟流水线，并通过引入核数据处理与蒙特卡罗案例，将其定位从多相模拟套件扩展为多物理模拟套件。

Method: 采用 agentic Rust 移植 NJOY 和 OpenMC；对 U-238 进行多普勒展宽截面计算并与 OpenMC 预生成 HDF5 文件比较；使用 ICSBEP 基准和作者博士论文中的 TRISO 燃料 FHR 球床对 OpenMC 蒙特卡罗移植进行初步测试。

Result: 实现了 NJOY 与 OpenMC 的 Rust 移植，展示了 U-238 多普勒展宽截面并与 OpenMC HDF5 结果对比；完成了 ICSBEP 基准及 FHR TRISO 球床的初步蒙特卡罗测试。代码仍为原型，尚需大量验证与确认。

Conclusion: Outram Park 加入核数据与蒙特卡罗案例后，从多相模拟套件转向多物理模拟套件；未来需开展更广泛的验证与确认研究。

Abstract: An agentic Rust port of both Njoy and OpenMC was achieved to produce a convenient ENDF to Monte Carlo simulation pipeline in Outram Park. The code is still a prototype with much more extensive verification and validation studies required in future studies. Doppler-broadened cross sections of U-238 are demonstrated in this paper and compared to OpenMC pregenerated HDF5 files. Additionally, initial testing of the Monte Carlo ports from OpenMC were also performed with ICSBEP benchmarks and a FHR Pebble with TRISO Fuel used in the author's PhD dissertation. The addition of nuclear data and Monte Carlo case studies shifts the focus of Outram Park from a Multi-Phase simulation kit to a Multi-Physics simulation kit.

</details>

### [67] [Localization Landscapes as Operator-Induced Geometry for Heterogeneous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13256)
*Josué García-Ávila*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 利用单次源求解得到的局域化景观与有效势 W_eff=1/u，将异质算子中的隐藏势垒、势阱和弱耦合区表示为算子感知的几何，用于解释低能模态、自适应感受野、注意力局部性和瓶颈敏感扩散；该方法有效但非普适。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质算子响应常由隐藏势垒、势阱和弱通信区主导，欧氏距离或原始图连接无法忠实描述，因此需要一种能反映算子约束与低能可达性的几何表示。

Method: 从离散薛定谔型算子与异质反应扩散算子出发，通过局域化景观的单次源求解构造 W_eff=1/u，并导出有效势阱、盆地划分与 Agmon 型邻域；进一步用强分区系数的有限元反应扩散算例验证连续异质系统。

Result: W_eff 几何可解释低能本征模的空间支撑，在固定图上产生自适应感受野，增强类注意力交互的局部性，并实现瓶颈敏感的扩散调制；有限元算例表明该框架可推广至异质连续介质。

Conclusion: 局域化景观几何在相关性受受限性、势垒和低能可达性控制时是强归纳偏置，但其收益依赖任务，并非普遍有效。

Abstract: Heterogeneous operators often organize response through hidden barriers, wells, and weakly communicating compartments that are not faithfully described by Euclidean distance or raw graph connectivity alone. We study this structure through the localization landscape, obtained from a single source solve, and the associated effective potential $W_{\mathrm{eff}} = 1/u$. Starting from both discrete Schr\"odinger-type operators and heterogeneous reaction-diffusion operators, we show how $W_{\mathrm{eff}}$ induces effective wells, basin partitions, and Agmon-type neighborhoods that serve as an operator-aware geometry for localization and transport. This geometry explains the spatial support of low-energy eigenmodes, produces adaptive receptive fields on a fixed graph, sharpens locality in attention-like interactions, and yields bottleneck-sensitive modulation of diffusion. We also include a finite-element reaction-diffusion example with strongly compartmentalized coefficients, demonstrating that the same framework extends beyond simple grid operators to heterogeneous continuum systems. The results support a precise conclusion: localization-landscape geometry is a strong inductive bias when relevance is controlled by confinement, barriers, and low-energy accessibility, but its benefits are task-dependent rather than universal.

</details>


### [68] [Fuzzy Erdos-Renyi random graphs: Spectral statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13432)
*J. A. Mendez-Bermudez*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文用随机矩阵理论（RMT）数值研究模糊Erdős-Rényi（ER）随机图的谱统计，发现平均度可作为其谱性质的标度参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊图和随机图的谱统计需要统一刻画，尤其当顶点与边的隶属度被随机化后，邻接矩阵变为稀疏随机矩阵，因此需要借助RMT理解不同图类型下的谱行为。

Method: 将模糊ER图模型中的顶点和边隶属度视为随机变量，构造稀疏随机邻接矩阵；针对无向/有向模糊图以及Hermitian/非Hermitian邻接矩阵系综，数值计算特征值与特征向量等标准RMT谱统计量。

Result: 数值研究表明，模糊ER图邻接矩阵的谱统计受ER模型平均度控制；平均度充当相应模糊设置中谱性质的缩放参数。

Conclusion: RMT方法可有效刻画模糊ER随机图的谱统计，且平均度是决定其谱性质标度的关键参数，为模糊网络的谱分析提供了依据。

Abstract: Within a random-matrix-theory (RMT) approach, we perform a detailed numerical study of the spectral statistics of fuzzy Erdos-Renyi (ER) random graphs. Specifically, we consider the membership values of the vertices and edges of a fuzzy ER graph model as random variables. In this way, the corresponding adjacency matrices become sparse matrices with random entries. Then, we apply standard RMT measures to characterize the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of ensembles of Hermitian and non-Hermitian adjacency matrices of both undirected and directed fuzzy graphs. We demonstrate that the average degree of the ER model serves as the scaling parameter of the spectral properties of the corresponding fuzzy setups.

</details>


### [69] [On Rare Nonresonant Regions and Subdiffusive Transport in an Interacting Disordered Quantum Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13834)
*A. Scardicchio,S. L. Sondhi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 通过直接数值实现迭代Schrieffer-Wolff构造，研究一维无序量子自旋链中稀有非共振区、其尺度生存概率及对亚扩散输运的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期关于亚扩散输运的构造性证明依赖指数稀有但参数上很长的非共振区，并要求跨尺度失败率可求和；因此需要独立、物理透明的数值检验，并量化证明中常数的保守程度。

Method: 在有限系统上逐尺度执行Schrieffer-Wolff变换，计算无序实现持续保持非共振的生存概率，同时监测算符增殖和缀饰局域可观测量的空间局域化；再将生存概率分解到各尺度，得到连续Schrieffer-Wolff步骤的失败率并与解析结果比较。

Result: 在数值可达范围内，生存概率至多随系统尺寸指数衰减；第一步失败率与解析结果高度一致，更高尺度的失败率快速下降；在最小耦合下，生存概率比严格下界高6至7个数量级。

Conclusion: 结果支持形成指数稀有但参数上很长的非共振区所需的求和机制，表明证明所用常数极为保守但其物理机制在可达尺度上可定量观测；直接数值实现可作为对复杂构造性证明的独立且物理透明的检验，并随机器辅助证明的普及而更有价值。

Abstract: We study rare nonresonant regions in a canonical one-dimensional disordered quantum spin chain by directly implementing the iterated Schrieffer-Wolff construction underlying the recent work of De Roeck, Giacomin, Huveneers and Prosniak on subdiffusive transport. For finite systems, we compute the probability that a disorder realization remains nonresonant through successive scales of the flow, while separately monitoring operator proliferation and the spatial localization of dressed local observables. The survival probability decays at most exponentially with system size over the numerically accessible regime. Resolving this decay scale by scale yields failure rates associated with successive Schrieffer-Wolff steps. The first rate is obtained analytically in excellent agreement with numerics, while higher-scale rates decrease rapidly throughout the regime studied. These results support the summability mechanism required for exponentially rare but parametrically long nonresonant regions, which provide the insulating bottlenecks responsible for subdiffusive transport. At the smallest couplings studied, the survival probability under our conditions exceeds the rigorous lower bound by six to seven orders of magnitude, demonstrating how conservative the constants required by the proof are while confirming that its underlying physical mechanism is quantitatively visible at accessible scales. More broadly, our results show how direct numerical implementations can provide an independent and physically transparent test of technically demanding constructive proofs, a methodology that may become increasingly useful as machine-assisted proofs become more common.

</details>


### [70] [Sequential Retrieval in Dense Associative Memory: Asymptotic Dynamics and Storage Capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13987)
*Mao Mishima,Ayaka Sakata,Kazushi Mimura*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 研究n体非对称稠密联想记忆的序列检索动力学：通过移动高阶Hebbian相互作用中的模式索引实现序列检索，检索态对应稳定极限环吸引子；用生成泛函分析推导动力学，发现延迟自相互作用消失，且当n≥3时归一化存储容量随相互作用阶数单调下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非对称高阶联想记忆模型如何实现序列检索，并刻画其存储容量随相互作用阶数的变化；同时对比对称Modern Hopfield模型的容量行为。

Method: 构建n体非对称稠密联想记忆模型，将序列检索实现为高阶Hebbian交互中模式索引的平移；采用生成泛函分析推导序列检索态的动力学方程，并与Amari-Maginu型信噪分析进行对照。

Result: 模型展现出对存储模式的序列检索，其检索态为稳定极限环吸引子；存储容量定义为该极限环保持稳定的最大加载率。生成泛函分析表明，非对称模型中延迟自相互作用项消失，所得重叠动力学可由Amari-Maginu型信噪分析重现。对于相互作用阶数n≥3，归一化存储容量随n增加而单调下降，这与对称Modern Hopfield模型形成对比。

Conclusion: 非对称高阶Hebbian交互可支持序列检索，但其容量行为与对称Modern Hopfield模型显著不同：在n≥3时容量随相互作用阶数增加而降低，揭示了非对称性对检索动力学和存储容量的关键影响。

Abstract: We study the retrieval dynamics of an $n$-body asymmetric dense associative memory model, in which sequential retrieval is implemented by shifting one pattern index in the higher-order Hebbian interaction. The model exhibits sequential retrieval of the stored patterns, and the retrieval state corresponds to a stable limit-cycle attractor. The storage capacity is defined as the maximum loading rate for which this limit cycle remains stable. Using generating functional analysis, we derive the dynamical equations describing the sequential retrieval state. The generating functional analysis reveals that the retarded self-interaction term vanishes in the asymmetric model, and the resulting overlap dynamics is reproduced by an Amari-Maginu-type signal-to-noise analysis. Also, we find that for the interaction order $n\ge 3$, the normalized storage capacity decreases monotonically with increasing interaction order, in contrast to symmetric Modern Hopfield models.

</details>


### [71] [OFC-like Behavior in Experimental Granular Piles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14166)
*K. Duplat,A. Douin,E. Saurety,G. Simon,E. Altshuler K. J. Måløy,O. Cochet-Escartin,O. Ramos*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 在二维颗粒实验中，增加颗粒间摩擦/耗散会使雪崩尺寸指数从1.58增至1.83，而上截止仍正比于系统尺寸；实验还表现出与OFC模型相似的非永久临界性和大事件可预测性，表明真实耗散系统中可出现τ>3/2的标度不变雪崩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为耗散会抑制临界性和大尺度事件，但OFC模型非保守区显示雪崩尺寸指数非普适且可超过平均场值3/2，系统尺度事件仍能持续。作者希望检验这些反常特征是否也存在于真实耗散颗粒系统中，并评估OFC框架对真实标度不变动力学的适用性。

Method: 采用二维颗粒系统，通过增加颗粒间摩擦来增强耗散；测量雪崩尺寸分布、标度指数和上截止随系统尺寸的变化，并将实验结果与OFC模型在记忆效应、局域动力学及大事件可预测性方面进行比较。

Result: 随摩擦/耗散增加，雪崩尺寸指数由τ=1.58升至τ=1.83，而最大雪崩上截止仍与系统尺寸成正比。实验与OFC模型在记忆效应、局部动力学以及大事件优于随机猜测的可预测性上相似，表明系统并非永久临界，而是在具有不同极端事件倾向的构型间演化。

Conclusion: OFC样动力学并非某一模型的特有反常现象，而可能为理解真实耗散系统中τ>3/2的标度不变雪崩动力学提供相关理论框架。

Abstract: Scale-invariant avalanche dynamics are commonly associated with criticality and robust, universal size exponents. Dissipation is generally expected to drive the system away from the critical point, progressively suppressing large events. The Olami--Feder--Christensen (OFC) model challenges this picture: in its non-conservative regime, the avalanche-size exponent is non-universal and can exceed the mean-field value $\tau=3/2$, while system-spanning events persist even at large dissipation. Here, we show that these apparently anomalous features are also observed experimentally in a two-dimensional granular system displaying scale-invariant avalanche dynamics. By increasing interparticle friction, and therefore dissipation, the avalanche-size exponent increases from $\tau=1.58$ to $\tau=1.83$, while the upper cutoff remains proportional to the system size. We further identify similarities between experiment and the OFC model in their memory effects, local dynamics, and the emergence of better-than-random predictability of large events. The latter indicates that the system does not remain permanently critical, but instead evolves through configurations with different propensities to generate extreme events. Our results suggest that OFC-like dynamics are not merely an anomalous feature of a particular model, but may provide a relevant framework for understanding scale-invariant dynamics with $\tau>3/2$ in real dissipative systems.

</details>


### [72] [Anderson localization in periodic elastic systems with random perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14410)
*Xin Feng,Peijun Li,Wei Wu*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文研究带随机扰动的亚波长弹性周期系统中的Anderson局域化；无扰动时用层势法将特征值问题化为边界积分方程，导出亚波长特征值渐近公式并证明亚波长带上方带隙；有扰动时用Floquet变换得到周期形式并导出共振频率方程，数值验证单体和二聚体，并展示随扰动增强与增多出现Anderson局域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解弹性超材料中的亚波长局域化提供数学基础，并揭示随机扰动下周期弹性系统的带隙结构与局域化机制。

Method: 无扰动系统：采用层势技术将特征值问题重述为边界积分方程，推导亚波长特征值渐近公式，证明带隙存在。扰动系统：应用Floquet变换获得周期性表述，导出一般扰动下决定共振频率的方程，并对扰动周期单体和二聚体开展数值实验。

Result: 无扰动系统存在亚波长带及其上方带隙；扰动下共振频率可由所得方程确定，数值结果与理论预测一致；增大随机扰动的强度和数量可观察到Anderson局域化。

Conclusion: 该工作为弹性超材料中亚波长局域化提供了数学理论支撑，表明随机扰动可诱导并增强Anderson局域化。

Abstract: This paper investigates Anderson localization in subwavelength elastic periodic systems with random perturbations. For the unperturbed system, we use layer potential techniques to reformulate the eigenvalue problem as boundary integral equations, derive asymptotic formulas for the subwavelength eigenvalues, and prove the existence of a band gap above the subwavelength band. For perturbed systems, we apply the Floquet transform to obtain a periodic formulation and derive equations determining the resonant frequencies under general perturbations. Numerical experiments for perturbed periodic monomers and dimers agree with the theoretical predictions. We further demonstrate Anderson localization by increasing the strength and number of random perturbations. These results provide a mathematical foundation for understanding subwavelength localization in elastic metamaterials.

</details>


### [73] [Statistical Landscape Exploration and Weak-Field Defects in Selective Oscillator Ising Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14550)
*Ömer Önder,Aydın Cem Keser*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文用SK自旋玻璃模型研究振荡器伊辛机(OIM)生成系综的统计结构，提出结合能量淘汰与副本复制的选择性方案OIM+；OIM+保留非平凡低能构型系综，但受稀疏弱局域场残余缺陷限制，短Metropolis-Hastings精修可高效修正，并在反铁磁偏置SK与G-set Max-Cut上取得有竞争力结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振荡器伊辛机为组合优化提供模拟动力学方法，但其生成解系综的统计结构尚不清楚；需要理解其探索的低能区域、缺陷特征及如何与局部精修结合。

Method: 基于Sherrington-Kirkpatrick(SK)自旋玻璃，提出OIM+：在连续退火轮次中引入基于能量的淘汰与副本重填充；用重叠分布、Hamming距离、层次聚类和局域场统计刻画状态；并施加短Metropolis-Hastings精修，与纯Metropolis长退火比较，还在反铁磁偏置SK系综和标准G-set Max-Cut基准上测试。

Result: OIM+反复选择后仍能探索结构化低能区域并保留非平凡构型系综；其特征性限制是稀疏的弱局域场残余缺陷。短Metropolis-Hastings精修优先修正这些缺陷，说明振荡器动力学主要负责集体探索，剩余修正以局部为主。弱局域场主要源于竞争相互作用项之间的相消，而非耦合普遍较弱。反铁磁偏置SK中混合方法在汇聚平均能量上可与显著更长的纯Metropolis退火相比；完整协议在G-set Max-Cut上也具竞争力。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种自然分工：模拟集体搜索负责主导性全局探索，针对性局部精修负责清除残余缺陷；OIM+与混合策略为提升振荡器伊辛机优化性能提供了可理解且有效的路径。

Abstract: Oscillator Ising machines (OIMs) provide an analog dynamical approach to combinatorial optimization, but comparatively little is known about the statistical structure of the ensembles they generate. Using the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick (SK) spin-glass model, we introduce a selective oscillator scheme, OIM+, in which successive annealing epochs are combined with energy-based elimination and repopulation of replicas. We characterize the resulting states through overlap distributions, Hamming distances, hierarchical clustering, and local-field statistics. OIM+ explores structured low-energy regions while retaining a nontrivial ensemble of configurations despite repeated selection. We identify sparse weak-local-field residual defects as a characteristic limitation of the dynamics. A short Metropolis-Hastings refinement preferentially corrects these defects, indicating that the oscillator dynamics performs the dominant collective exploration while the remaining corrections are predominantly local. The weak fields arise mainly from cancellation among competing interaction terms rather than uniformly weak couplings. The same behavior persists for an antiferromagnetically biased SK ensemble, where the hybrid method reaches a pooled mean energy comparable to a substantially longer pure Metropolis anneal. The complete protocol also achieves competitive performance on standard G-set Max-Cut benchmarks. These results suggest a natural division of labor between collective analog search and targeted local refinement.

</details>


### [74] [Why 2D Models Fail to Capture Flow Properties in 3D Heterogeneous Media: A Connectivity Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14609)
*Cristian Picighelli,Ivan Colecchio,Marcelo Piva,Alejandro D. Otero,Alejandro Boschan*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文研究二维和三维非均质介质中等效水力传导系数 K_eq 随粗化尺度 λ/I 的变化，比较 HCS、ICS、LCS、NS 等不同连通性结构，并用随机方法刻画 P[log(K_eq)] 及其矩。结果显示 2D 与 3D 存在显著差异，连通性使 K_eq 呈幂律依赖，并揭示用 2D 模型表征 3D 流动的严重局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化空间连通性和空间维度如何控制宏观等效水力传导系数，检验多高斯介质粗化解析式在低连通性和高连通性介质中的适用性，并说明二维模型替代三维流动模型的局限性。

Method: 设局部水力传导系数 k(r) 服从对数正态分布，构造 2D/3D 中不同连通性样本：高连通 HCS、中间/多高斯 ICS、低连通 LCS 和无结构 NS。采用随机方法计算 P[log(K_eq)] 及其均值 ⟨K_eq⟩、对数方差 σ²_log(K_eq)、对数偏度 γ_log(K_eq) 随 λ/I 的变化，并用关键路径分析量化连通性。

Result: 发现 2D 与 3D 的 K_eq 标度行为显著不同，可归因于两个与空间维度相关的关键因素。与 ICS 相比，LCS 和 HCS 中现有多高斯粗化解析式不适用，且 P[log(K_eq)] 在所有 λ/I 下均偏离高斯分布。关键路径分析表明 K_eq 与连通性呈幂律关系；即使地质统计参数相同，连通性也可导致 2D 与 3D 宏观流动性质显著不同。

Conclusion: 连通性与空间维度共同决定等效水力传导系数及其分布。二维模型可能严重歪曲三维非均质介质中的宏观流动性质，因此需要采用三维框架并显式考虑连通性。

Abstract: We analyze how the equivalent hydraulic conductivity, $K_{eq}$, varies with the coarsening scale $\lambda/I$ in 2D and 3D heterogeneous media. Their local hydraulic conductivity, $k({\bf r})$, follows a lognormal distribution in all cases, while spatial connectivity of $k({\bf r})$ ranges from high (HCS), through intermediate or multi-Gaussian (ICS) and low (LCS), to unstructured (NS). Using a stochastic approach, we characterize the full distribution $P[\log(K_{eq})]$, and its moments: mean $\langle K_{eq}\rangle$, log-variance $\sigma_{\log(K_{eq})}^{2}$, and log-skewness $\gamma_{\log(K_{eq})}$, as a function of $\lambda/I$. Significant contrasts appear between 2D and 3D, which we interpret in terms of two key factors associated with spatial dimensionality. We observe that, compared with ICS, LCS and HCS exhibit two distinct features: existing analytical expressions for coarse-graining of $k({\bf r})$ in multi-Gaussian media do not apply, while $P[\log(K_{eq})]$ deviates from Gaussian at all scales $\lambda/I$. Critical path analysis is then used to quantify the connectivity of our samples, and show that $K_{eq}$ exhibits a power-law dependence on it. Our results reveal that connectivity can lead to substantial differences between 2D and 3D macroscopic flow properties, even for the same geostatistical parameters, highlighting the severe limitations of using 2D models to represent 3D flows.

</details>

*Adithya J D,Ranjan Modak,Shaon Sahoo*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文研究准周期势中决定局域化性质的关键参数，指出除关联外，偏置也至关重要；通过可调偏置与关联的扩展 Aubry-André 模型，发现偏置控制退局域态比例，并给出两个临界偏置阈值；零偏置关联模型仍表现为 Anderson 型全局域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Anderson 局域化中，一维任意非关联无序会局域化所有本征态，而一维准周期势具有更丰富的局域化行为；核心问题是哪些势特征控制局域化性质。

Result: 偏置与关联共同影响局域化，但偏置是决定退局域态比例的关键。势强度增加通常增强局域化趋势，同时增强准周期势关联；该反直觉行为可由偏置解释：势强度增加降低偏置，从而有利于局域化。对所用哈密顿量族发现两个临界偏置阈值：低于较低阈值时整个谱局域；低于较高阈值时至多一部分态可退局域，不能全谱退局域。零偏置模型尽管关联，仍在极弱势强下使所有态局域，恢复 Anderson 型情景。

Abstract: Unlike in Anderson localization - where any amount of uncorrelated disorder localizes all eigenstates in one dimension - a one-dimensional system with quasiperiodic potential supports a richer range of localization behavior. This paper investigates the fundamental question of which potential characteristics govern localization properties. We characterize quasiperiodic potentials using two independent parameters, correlation and bias, and demonstrate that bias, in addition to correlation, critically influences localization. Through the study of an extended Aubry-Andr\'{e} model, in which a tunable parameter allows systematic control over both the bias and correlation of the potential, we show that bias is key in determining the fraction of delocalized states. An increase in the potential strength generally enhances the tendency toward localization, while simultaneously strengthening the correlations in the quasiperiodic potential. This apparent counterintuitive behavior can be understood in terms of the bias parameter: increasing the potential strength reduces the bias, which in turn favors localization. For the family of Hamiltonians considered, we identify two critical bias thresholds: below the lower threshold, the entire spectrum is localized, whereas below the higher threshold, at most a fraction of the states can be delocalized, precluding delocalization of the entire spectrum. To further test our framework, we examine a quasiperiodic model with zero bias and find that, despite its correlated nature, all states become localized even at very weak potential strengths - recovering the Anderson-like scenario.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望系统性地设计无序场，使其承载预先指定的离域本征态，并理解非厄米系统中谱结构、局域化与无序之间的可控关系，同时推广到任意维度、任意跃迁场以及厄米/非厄米体系。

Result: Hatano-Nelson 链的谱形成复平面环，环参量类似动量并可作为好量子数；本征态表现为次指数局域化，log|ψ|在空间随机游走并由 ξ_sel 表征。M=2 等权实二元无序给出 Cassini 卵形谱环，且在弱于由跃迁强度设定的临界无序时，两个本征态的次指数局域化长度发散。若任意 M 和权重的无序场位于半径等于跃迁强度的圆上，圆心属于谱并位于某一谱环上，对应平面波型本征态且 ξ_sel 发散。文中还构造了一维链：在 Anderson 局域本征态中存在一个带随机相位的完全离域本征态，局域化长度在接近该态时按 1/|E|^{2/3} 发散。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种系统构造无序场的方法，可植入具有任意性质的预设本征态，为厄米和非厄米系统中谱工程与局域化调控提供了通用框架。

Abstract: We derive disorder fields tailored by the details of a choice of a delocalized wave function. We first investigate the unidirectional Hatano-Nelson chain and its localization properties under $M$-base diagonal disorder with variable weights. The spectrum forms loops in the complex plane and the loop parameter is a good quantum number similar to a momentum. All eigenstates are subexponentially `localized', i.e. the logarithm of the absolute value of the wave function performs a random walk in space, and are characterized by a corresponding length scale $\xi_{sel}$ as shown in 1998 by Silvestrov for the general Hatano-Nelson chain. For $M=2$ real-valued binary disorder with equal weights the model was solved in Zeitschrift f\"ur Naturforschung A 81 421, yielding Cassini oval spectral loops and a diverging subexponential localization length for two eigenstates and for disorder weaker than a critical value set by the hopping strength. When the disorder field for any $M$ and arbitrary weights is confined to circles in the complex plane with radius equal to the hopping strength, the circle center will belong to the spectrum and to one of the spectral loops, and host a plane-wave-like eigenstate with diverging $\xi_{sel}$. We generalize to tailoring on-site disorder for a given eigenstate at a given energy, for any lattice dimension, and any hopping field (both ordered and disordered), for Hermitian and non-Hermitian systems. We exemplify by constructing a one-dimensional chain with Anderson-localized eigenstates hosting a completely delocalized one with random phases. The localization length diverges as $1/|E|^{2/3}$ upon approaching the delocalized state. Our method can be used for the systematic construction of disorder fields which host predefined eigenstates with arbitrary properties.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近多体局域化交叉中，局部记忆在不同本征态和系综实现间强烈涨落，观测到的异质性混合了多个统计层次的贡献，因此需要分离涨落来源并理解其空间组织。

Method: 构建构型空间距离分布的统计框架，通过方差分解分离同一本征态内、同一样本不同本征态间、样本间涨落，并分析空间协方差；应用于具有相同单点场边缘分布的随机余弦和准周期余弦 Ising 系综，并推广到固定磁化自旋链的约束构型空间。

Result: 在精确对角化尺寸下，最清晰差异出现在无序实现或准周期相位样本之间，而本征态内与同一样本本征态间贡献大致相当。随机系综外层涨落具有更强均匀分量，准周期相位涨落更集中在有限波数并在控制距离中心的空间平均中部分抵消。随机系综交叉附近，系综平均距离中心对名义场强变化敏感，结合实现场幅的样本差异可解释距离中心样本间变化的大部分；半链纠缠的样本间涨落也反映幅度变化及其平均响应。

Conclusion: 同时解析统计层次和空间模式，可更完整刻画样本依赖的多体记忆及其构型空间概率几何，并适用于约束构型空间。

Abstract: Near many-body localization crossovers, local memory fluctuates strongly among eigenstates and ensemble realizations, but the observed heterogeneity
  combines contributions from different statistical levels. We develop a statistical framework based on configuration-space distance distributions that uses
  a variance decomposition to separate fluctuations within eigenstates, between eigenstates of one sample, and between samples. Applying this framework to
  random-cosine and quasiperiodic-cosine Ising ensembles with the same one-site field marginal, we find that, at the exact-diagonalization sizes studied,
  the clearest difference occurs among disorder realizations or quasiperiodic phase samples, whereas within-eigenstate and within-sample eigenstate-to-
  eigenstate contributions remain broadly comparable. Spatial covariances show that random outer fluctuations have a much stronger uniform component,
  whereas quasiperiodic phase fluctuations are organized more strongly at finite wave number and partly cancel in the spatial average controlling the
  distance center. We find that, near the crossover in the random ensemble, the ensemble-averaged distance center is particularly sensitive to changes in
  the nominal field strength. Combined with sample-to-sample differences in the realized field amplitude, this mean response accounts for much of the
  sample-to-sample variation in the distance center. Analysis of half-chain entanglement further shows that its sample-to-sample fluctuations likewise
  reflect the combined effects of amplitude variations and its own mean response. An application to a fixed-magnetization spin chain demonstrates the
  framework in a constrained configuration space. Resolving both statistical level and spatial mode therefore provides a more complete picture of sample-
  dependent many-body memory and its configuration-space probability geometry.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解可积自旋链中畴壁扩散的普适波前动力学，特别是线性自旋波图像下色散关系如何控制波前传播、展宽以及各向异性诱导的定性转变。

Method: 从微观可积Hamiltonian导出长波极限磁振子色散；在线性自旋波描述基础上加入领头非线性修正，得到立方色散项；并用完整非线性自旋动力学数值模拟验证解析标度。

Result: 色散从各向同性点的二次型过渡到易面区的线性型。各向同性极限下二次色散导致扩散式展宽；易面区畴壁前沿弹道传播。立方色散项产生t^{1/3}展宽。立方修正符号在临界各向异性Δ_c=1/7（γ_c≈1.047）改变，使色散尾迹由拖尾反转为超前。数值模拟证实解析标度，并显示振幅无关自旋波前沿与振幅相关非线性孤子传播共存。

Conclusion: 畴壁波前动力学可由长波磁振子色散及其非线性色散修正统一刻画；临界各向异性处存在波尾方向反转，且线性自旋波前沿与非线性孤子传播可在同一可积系统中共存。

Abstract: We study the far-from-equilibrium dynamics of domain-wall initial states in the integrable lattice Landau--Lifshitz spin chain. Building on the linear spin-wave description of domain-wall spreading, we derive the long-wavelength magnon dispersion directly from the microscopic integrable Hamiltonian and use it to characterize the propagation and broadening of the resulting wavefronts. The dispersion interpolates between a quadratic form at the isotropic point and a linear form in the easy-plane regime. In the isotropic limit, the quadratic dispersion leads to diffusive broadening, whereas in the easy-plane regime the domain-wall front propagates ballistically. Keeping the leading nonlinear correction to the linear dispersion gives a cubic dispersive term, which produces a characteristic $t^{1/3}$ broadening of the ballistic front. We further show that the sign of this cubic correction changes at a critical anisotropy, leading to a reversal of the dispersive wake from trailing to advancing. The resulting critical point occurs at $\Delta_c=1/7$, corresponding to $\gamma_c\simeq 1.047$. Numerical simulations of the full nonlinear spin dynamics confirm the analytical scaling and demonstrate the coexistence of the amplitude-independent spin-wave front with amplitude-dependent nonlinear soliton propagation.

</details>

### [79] [Can Autonomous LLM Agents Execute Multireference Quantum Chemistry Calculations?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13357)
*Victor Chang Lee,James M. Rondinelli*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究自主大语言模型（LLM）智能体能否在无人工干预下执行多参考电子结构计算工作流，包括活性空间选择、态平均、收敛恢复和态识别；结果表明，结构化决策框架可显著提升覆盖率与精度，并在完整流程信息下高保真复现QUEST计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参考电子结构计算难以自动化，因为活性空间选择、态平均、收敛恢复和态识别等关键流程决策通常依赖专家判断。

Method: 构建自主LLM智能体，利用文献类比或明确记录的化学推理选择活性空间，生成并提交ORCA计算，分析输出，并将所有决策记录在可审计推理日志中；以QUESTDB的558个垂直跃迁能（VTE）为基准，并对比无引导基线、结构化决策阶梯及完整工作流信息条件。

Result: 无引导基线智能体覆盖率为24.9%，平均绝对误差（MAE）为0.373 eV；引入结构化决策阶梯后覆盖率提升至44.1%，MAE降至0.339 eV，双激发和Rydberg激发增益最大；在提供完整工作流信息时，智能体以23 meV的MAE复现已发表QUEST计算，并在七次尝试内解决75%的目标构型。

Conclusion: 当代LLM智能体能够自主执行并复现复杂的多参考量子化学工作流，而结构化推理框架对于实现可靠的高通量、高保真电子结构计算至关重要。

Abstract: Multireference electronic-structure calculations remain difficult to automate because critical workflow decisions, including active-space selection, state averaging, convergence recovery, and state identification, traditionally rely on expert judgment. Here, we investigate whether an autonomous large language model (LLM) agent can perform these tasks without human intervention. The agent selects active spaces using literature-grounded analogies or explicitly documented chemical reasoning, generates and submits ORCA calculations, analyzes outputs, and records all decisions in an auditable reasoning log. Benchmarking against 558 vertical transition energies (VTEs) from QUESTDB shows that an unguided baseline agent achieves 24.9% coverage with a mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.373 eV. Introducing a structured decision ladder increases coverage to 44.1% while reducing the MAE to 0.339 eV. The largest gains are observed for double and Rydberg excitations, demonstrating that expert-informed procedural guidance substantially improves active-space construction and state identification. When provided with complete workflow information, the agent successfully reproduces published QUEST calculations with an MAE of only 23 meV, resolving 75% of target configurations within seven attempts. These results demonstrate that contemporary LLM agents can autonomously execute and reproduce complex multireference quantum-chemical workflows, while highlighting the importance of structured reasoning frameworks for achieving reliable high-throughput and high-fidelity electronic-structure calculations.

</details>


### [80] [Automated AFGL quantum number assignment for CO$_2$ isotopologues using a graph neural network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13803)
*Marco G. Barnfield,Sergei N. Yurchenko,Jonathan Tennyson*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出自动化流程，用GraphSAGE图神经网络为CO2的12种稳定同位素、15000 cm⁻¹以下计算振转态分配AFGL量子数，并结合匈牙利算法与五轮自举提升覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确分配计算分子能级的量子数是生成全面谱线列表及辐射转移所需谱线增宽参数的关键瓶颈。

Method: 以MARVEL经验能级进行直推式训练GraphSAGE；利用同位素间扰动链和同位素内转动阶梯边传播标签；在同多联、同转动量子数、同宇称状态组内用匈牙利算法局部强制物理唯一性；通过五代bootstrap将高置信预测加入训练集；并引入决策树将非对称同位素体的AFGL转换为Herzberg记号。

Result: 在12种同位素体上为224,650个此前未标记态分配量子数，占全部可用态（含MARVEL导出能级）的10.7%，使5000 cm⁻¹以下覆盖率提升至97.4%。

Conclusion: 该架构和唯一性约束可推广到CO2之外，适用于具有守恒多联类量子数且存在大量未标记计算态的分子体系，为下一代大规模计算谱线列表的自动量子数标注提供路径。

Abstract: Accurate quantum number assignment for calculated molecular energy levels is a critical bottleneck in generating line broadening parameters for comprehensive line lists for radiative transfer applications. We present an automated pipeline for assigning Air Force Geophysics Laboratory (AFGL) quantum numbers to CO2 calculated rovibrational states lying below 15,000cm$^{-1}$ across all 12 stable isotopologues. A GraphSAGE graph neural network is trained transductively on empirical (MARVEL) energy levels, exploiting inter-isotopologue perturbation chains and intra-isotopologue rotational ladder edges to propagate assignment information to unlabelled calculated states. Physical uniqueness is enforced locally by a Hungarian algorithm solver operating within groups of states sharing the same polyad, rotational quantum number, and parity. A five-generation bootstrap loop iteratively promotes high-confidence predictions into the training set, expanding coverage without additional labelling effort. The pipeline assigns 224,650 previously unlabelled states over 12 isotopologues, accounting for 10.7$\%$ of all available states (including MARVEL-derived levels), with coverage now increased to 97.4$\%$ below 5000cm$^{-1}$. The pipeline includes a novel decision tree method for converting AFGL to Herzberg notation in asymmetric isotopologues, while the architecture and Hungarian uniqueness enforcement are applicable beyond CO2, any molecular system with a conserved polyad-like quantum number and a large body of unlabelled computed states is a natural target for this approach, suggesting a pathway toward automated quantum number annotation for the next generation of large-scale computed line lists.

</details>


### [81] [Accelerating ab initio spin-phonon relaxation simulation of single-ion magnets by quantum embedding and spatial truncation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14371)
*Yifan Deng,Zhe-Bin Guan,Zilong Zou,Zheng Sun,Ze-Wei Li,Bingwu Wang,Hong Jiang*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将DMET与CASSCF结合并引入空间截断，用于Dy³⁺基单离子磁体的静态磁性质和自旋-声子耦合参数计算，证明截断至第一配位圈附近可在几乎不损失精度的前提下大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单离子磁体在高密度存储和量子计算中具有前景，但预测自旋-声子耦合与磁弛豫通常需要大量非平衡多组态计算，计算成本高，因此需要发展准确且高效的计算框架。

Method: 采用密度矩阵嵌入理论（DMET）与完整活性空间自洽场（CASSCF）相结合，对Dy³⁺基单离子磁体的静态磁性质和自旋-声子耦合（SPC）参数进行基准测试；进一步结合空间截断策略计算SPC参数。

Result: 结果表明，将空间截断至第一配位圈附近可显著降低计算成本，同时对有效能垒和弛豫时间尺度的误差影响可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 该研究为准确、高效地预测自旋动力学提供了实用计算框架，并为高性能单分子磁体的理性设计奠定了基础。

Abstract: Single-ion magnets (SIMs) show promise for high-density storage and quantum computing, but predicting spin-phonon coupling (SPC) and magnetic relaxation remains challenging due to the need for numerous non-equilibrium multiconfigurational calculations. Recent advances in quantum embedding methods offer a potential route to address this issue. In this work, density matrix embedding theory (DMET) combined with complete active space self-consistent field (CASSCF) is benchmarked for the static magnetic properties and spin-phonon coupling (SPC) parameters of Dy$^{3+}$-based SIMs. The method is further combined with spatial truncation to calculate SPC parameters for these SIMs. It is found that truncating the space near the first coordination sphere reduces the computational cost dramatically while keeping the errors in the effective energy barrier and relaxation time-scale negligible. This study provides a practical calculation framework for accurate and efficient spin dynamics prediction, laying the foundation for the rational design of high-performance single-molecule magnets.

</details>


### [82] [From Transition-State Geometry to Gap Times: What Lagrangian Betweenness Measures in Chemical Reaction Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14378)
*Stephen Wiggins*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该文澄清了 Lagrangian betweenness (LB) 在已知过渡态几何下的含义：它刻画由过渡态组织的输运，而非流形本身或分子停留时间的通用指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学过渡态是相空间瓶颈，轨迹沿稳定方向接近、沿不稳定方向离开；LB 结合后向与前向形变，可能反映这种“聚集-发散”几何。作者追问当过渡态几何已知时，LB 究竟度量什么。

Method: 针对线性秩一鞍点解析 LB 的空间分布及非线性修正；在固定空间分辨率与增长观测时间两种极限下考察 LB；用可分离四次哈密顿量显式展示，并用不可分离哈密顿量考察双曲周期轨道附近行为；最后以 HCN/CNH 异构化为实例，结合相空间过渡态理论给出的分界面和 gap time，并引入 incoming reactive action 进行动力学解释。

Result: 线性秩一鞍点处 LB 空间上恒定，尽管稳定/不稳定流形存在；缩小初始邻域时相对非线性修正很小，包括对数局域逃逸尺度上的观测时间。固定空间分辨率、增大观测时间时，LB 的空间变化可集中于稳定或不稳定流形附近。可分离四次模型显式体现两极限；不可分离模型显示双曲周期轨道附近局域平坦化。HCN/CNH 中，在出口前，较大的 LB 总体伴随更长的最终 gap time；初始条件越接近 normally hyperbolic invariant manifold，局域通过时间越长，通过期间累积拉伸越大。但在 0.5 ps 时仍在内、且 5 ps 前出口的轨迹中，早期 LB 不能有效排序出口时间。

Conclusion: 因此，LB 在该例中刻画过渡态组织的输运；大 LB 既不是内禀流形标记，也不是分子停留时间的通用度量。

Abstract: A chemical transition state is a phase-space bottleneck: trajectories approach along stable directions and leave along unstable directions. Lagrangian betweenness (LB), introduced in finite-time transport theory for fluid flows, combines backward and forward deformation in a way suggestive of this gather-and-disperse geometry. We ask what LB measures when the transition-state geometry is known. For a linear rank-one saddle, LB is spatially constant even though the stable and unstable manifolds are present. Relative nonlinear corrections remain small on shrinking initial neighborhoods, including observation times on the logarithmic local escape scale. At fixed spatial resolution, however, increasing observation time can concentrate the spatial variation of LB near a stable or unstable manifold. A separable quartic Hamiltonian makes these two limits explicit. A nonseparable Hamiltonian shows local flattening near a hyperbolic periodic orbit. We then use HCN/CNH isomerization, where dividing surfaces and gap times are established by phase-space transition-state theory. Before any exit, larger LB generally accompanies longer eventual gap times. The incoming reactive action provides a dynamical interpretation: initial conditions closer to the normally hyperbolic invariant manifold have longer local passages and greater accumulated stretching during passage. Among trajectories still inside at 0.5 ps that exit before 5 ps, early LB does not usefully rank exit times. LB therefore characterizes transition-state-organized transport in this example; a large value is neither an intrinsic manifold marker nor a general measure of molecular residence time.

</details>


### [83] [Compile-once operation graphs for reusable continuous unitary transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14831)
*Bo Peng,Niranjan Govind*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种“一次编译、多次复用”的数值运算图：将连续幺正变换中重复出现的算子代数关系预编译并持久化，供不同系数、附加算符、导数及CPU/GPU正反向计算复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续幺正变换在哈密顿量系数和可观测量演化时会反复计算同一算子代数，导致昂贵的算子代数生成被重复执行；核心动机是将算子代数生成与数值变换解耦，提高复用性并降低重复构造开销。

Method: 把算子代数关系编译为可保存、可重载的数值运算图，支持新系数集、附加算符、导数以及CPU/GPU上的正向与反向计算。构造阶段预先确定存储需求，并将生成的多体代数贡献直接写入最终磁盘后端表示，避免在内存中物化海量中间项。

Result: 对约化分子模型的氢、氘参数化复用同一编译图，无需重新生成算子方程。在包含超过3.5亿条生成贡献的诊断型压力测试中，峰值主机内存仅比最终存储尺寸高约7%。交叉验证表明，CPU与GPU实现、正向与反向操作以及重复加载编译图均能在数值精度内复现测试操作。

Conclusion: 该compile-once表示将昂贵的算子代数生成与复用它的数值变换分离，支持多参数化、多算符、导数及CPU/GPU正反向计算，并显著控制构造阶段的内存开销。

Abstract: Continuous unitary transformations repeatedly evaluate the same operator algebra as the coefficients of a Hamiltonian and its observables evolve. We compile these algebraic relationships once into a numerical operation graph that can be saved and reused for new coefficient sets, additional operators, derivatives, and forward and reverse calculations on CPUs and GPUs. Reuse is demonstrated by applying the same compiled graph to hydrogen and deuterium parameterizations of a reduced molecular model without regenerating the operator equations. The main computational challenge is construction: generated many-body algebra can contain hundreds of millions of contributions before numerical propagation begins. We address this by determining the required storage in advance and writing contributions directly into the final disk-backed representation. In a deliberately large diagnostic-enabled stress test containing more than 350 million generated contributions, peak host memory remains only about 7\% above the final stored size. Cross-checks show that CPU and GPU implementations, forward and reverse operations, and repeated loading of the compiled graph reproduce the tested operations to numerical precision. This compile-once representation separates expensive operator-algebra generation from the numerical transformations that reuse it.

</details>


### [84] [Exchange-Correlation Potentials and Energies from Inverse Generalized Kohn-Sham Calculations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15845)
*Vishal Subramanian,Bikash Kanungo,Vaibhav Khanna,Paul M. Zimmerman,Vikram Gavini*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过求解广义Kohn-Sham（GKS）逆问题，利用高精度关联从头算密度，获得了原子和分子的精确交换-关联（XC）势与能量，并揭示在强关联体系中KS与GKS的关联势和能量存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKS形式将DFT推广到可使用任意单电子算符，从而扩展XC泛函建模范围；但与KS理论不同，GKS缺乏对应的精确XC势和能量，严重阻碍了XC泛函的发展。

Method: 作者求解逆GKS问题，以高精度关联的从头算电子密度为输入，构造原子和分子的精确GKS XC势与能量，并在弱关联和强关联体系中进行验证。

Result: 该方法在弱关联与强关联体系中均得到验证；同时检验了“KS与GKS的关联势和能量相近”这一常见但未经充分验证的假设，发现在强关联体系中二者差异显著。

Conclusion: 该工作为在GKS-DFT框架内建模下一代XC泛函提供了有力工具。

Abstract: The Kohn-Sham (KS) formulation of density functional theory (DFT) is a map from the many-electron problem to an effective single-electron problem that is governed by a local multiplicative potential. The generalized-Kohn-Sham (GKS) formalism extends it to permit any single-electron operator---nonlocal, local non-multiplicative, local multiplicative, or any combination of them. Doing so expands the scope and ease of modeling the exchange-correlation (XC) functional in DFT, which encodes the complicated many-electron interactions into a mean-field of the electron density. However, unlike KS theory, development of XC functionals in GKS theory has been hindered by the absence of corresponding exact XC potentials and energies. We present the exact XC potentials and energies for atoms and molecules by solving the inverse GKS problem, using highly accurate correlated \textit{ab initio} densities. Our approach is validated across weakly and strongly correlated systems. We further examine a common, yet untested, assumption that KS and GKS correlation potentials and energies are similar, finding instead that they differ substantially in strongly correlated systems. Overall, this work offers a powerful tool to model next-generation of XC functionals within the GKS formalism of DFT.

</details>


### [85] [Toward Predictive Hydride Bond Energetics with Neural-Network Wavefunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15898)
*Aqsa Shaikh,Lubos Mitas,P. Ganesh,Jaron T. Krogel*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文评估基于 Psiformer 的神经网络变分蒙特卡洛（NN-VMC）在 TM-H 键解离能预测中的表现，并与 CCSDT(Q)/CBS 及现有理论/实验数据比较。结果表明 NN-VMC 对主族和早期过渡金属氢化物具有竞争力，而晚期过渡金属氢化物仍是挑战性基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡金属氢化物（TM-H）键解离能（BDE）的精确预测受强电子相关、相对论效应和核量子贡献影响，现有方法仍难以可靠处理。因此需要评估新型电子结构方法在复杂体系中的定量能力。

Method: 采用基于 Psiformer 波函数拟设的 NN-VMC，并使用统一的 ccECP 哈密顿量以便与从头算结果一致比较。研究对象包括 LiH、OH、TiH 和 NiH，基准为 CCSDT(Q)/CBS 及可得理论与实验数据。为降低有限训练误差，引入零方差外推和无限步外推方案。

Result: LiH 和 OH 中，NN-VMC 总能量与 CBS 外推从头算结果相差在亚毫 Hartree 级，BDE 差异小于 2σ。Ti 和 TiH 中，训练结束时变分 NN-VMC 能量已与 CCSD(T)/CBS 一致，训练后外推进一步逼近 CCSDT(Q)/CBS。NiH 中，增加 NN 波函数拟设的行列式数目可改善相关能恢复；其 BDE 的现有理论预测分为高、低 BDE 组，但实验数据分散较大，无法明确判定最优理论方法。

Conclusion: NN-VMC 结合 ccECP 哈密顿量为定量预测主族及早期 TM-H 能量学提供了有竞争力的框架；晚期 TM-H 则被识别为未来神经网络波函数和电子结构理论发展的重要基准体系。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of transition-metal hydride (TM-H) bond dissociation energies (BDEs) remains challenging because of strong electron correlation, relativistic effects, and nuclear quantum contributions. Here, we assess the performance of neural-network variational Monte Carlo (NN-VMC) based on the Psiformer ansatz for systems with increasing complexity (LiH, OH, TiH and NiH), and compare it against CCSDT(Q)/CBS as well as available theoretical and experimental data. Throughout this study, both NN-VMC and ab initio calculations employ a common ccECP Hamiltonian to enable tractable and consistent comparisons. To reduce finite-training errors, we introduce complementary zero-variance and infinite-step extrapolation schemes. For LiH and OH systems, NN-VMC yields total energies that differ within sub-milli Hartree compared to CBS extrapolated ab initio results and the BDE differences remain under 2$\sigma$. In case of Ti and TiH, the variational NN-VMC energies at the end of training are already consistent with CCSD(T)/CBS, while post-training extrapolation systematically closes the gap towards the CCSDT(Q)/CBS results. In contrast, NiH provides a stringent test of wavefunction expressivity, where increasing the number of determinants in NN-wavefunction ansatz improves the recovered correlation energy. The comparison of NiH BDE reveals that, while the existing theoretical predictions cluster into distinct high and low BDE groups, the broad scatter in available experimental data prevents a definitive assessment of the most accurate theoretical approach. This work demonstrates that NN-VMC with ccECPs Hamiltonian provides a competitive framework for quantitative prediction of main-group and early TM-H energetics, while identifying late TM-H as an important benchmark for future developments in neural-network wavefunctions and electronic structure theory.

</details>

### [86] [Strain-Induced Helical Superconductivity and the Zero-Field Diode Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13380)
*Raigo Nagashima,Jörg Schmalian*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 应变可通过混合主导s波与子主导双分量配对并增强对称性允许的Lifshitz耦合，在无磁性、无外磁场下诱导非互易超导输运，形成具有自发有限库珀对动量和时间反演破缺的螺旋超导态及超导二极管效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释PbTaSe2中应变诱导零场超导二极管效应的机制，并寻找无需磁性或外磁场即可产生非互易超导输运的途径。

Method: 理论分析单轴应变下主导s波与子主导两分量配对通道的混合及其Lifshitz耦合；研究超过临界应变后系统向螺旋超导态的转变，并考察二极管效应对电流方向、主应变反转和剪切应变的依赖。

Result: 超过临界应变后，耦合驱动系统进入自发选定有限库珀对动量且破缺时间反演对称性的螺旋态；二极管效应随应变非单调，平行与垂直于残余镜面的电流响应不同，并与实验一致。反向主应变使二极管方向切换90°，剪切应变则使其连续旋转。

Conclusion: 应变可作为对称性选择手段来产生、调控和诊断自发螺旋超导性，为无磁场超导二极管效应提供机制解释与可检验预测。

Abstract: Motivated by the strain-induced zero-field superconducting diode effect observed in PbTaSe$_2$, we identify a mechanism by which strain generates nonreciprocal superconducting transport without magnetism or an external magnetic field. Uniaxial strain mixes a dominant $s$-wave order parameter with a subdominant two-component pairing channel and enhances their symmetry-allowed Lifshitz coupling. Beyond a critical strain, this coupling drives a transition into a helical state with spontaneously selected finite Cooper-pair momentum and broken time-reversal symmetry. The resulting diode effect is generically non-monotonic in strain and exhibits distinct responses for currents parallel and perpendicular to the residual mirror plane, consistent with experiment. Our theory predicts that reversing the principal strain switches the diode direction by $90^\circ$, while shear strain rotates it continuously. These results establish strain as a symmetry-selective means of creating, controlling, and diagnosing spontaneous helical superconductivity.

</details>


### [87] [Superconducting Diode Effect in Josephson $φ_0$ Junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13399)
*A. Janalizadeh,Y. M. Shukrinov,M. R. Kolahchi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 研究铁磁弱连接约瑟夫森结中Rashba自旋轨道耦合下的超导二极管效应，指出强交换场下需引入高阶梯度项，二极管效应源于线性与高阶梯度项的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准φ0结模型仅适用于弱交换场 h ≲ Tc；实际铁磁势垒常处于 h ≫ Tc，传统Ginzburg-Landau梯度展开失效，需引入高阶梯度项以保证自由能稳定。

Method: 在自由能展开中加入高阶梯度项，并分析线性梯度耦合与高阶梯度项之间的 interplay。

Result: 线性梯度耦合单独会产生有限动量库珀配对，但不改变临界电流对称性；其与高阶梯度项共同作用则导致约瑟夫森二极管效应。

Conclusion: 在强铁磁交换场区域，高阶梯度项对自由能稳定性和超导二极管效应的出现至关重要，二极管效应来自线性梯度耦合与高阶梯度耦合的协同。

Abstract: We investigate the superconducting diode effect in a Josephson junction with a ferromagnetic weak link in the presence of Rashba spin-orbit coupling. While the standard $\varphi_0$ junction model is valid only for weak exchange fields $h \lesssim T_c$, realistic ferromagnetic barriers typically operate in the regime $h \gg T_c$, where the conventional Ginzburg-Landau gradient expansion becomes insufficient and requires the inclusion of higher order gradient terms to ensure stability of the free energy. By extending the free energy with such higher order term, we demonstrate that the linear gradient coupling alone leads to finite momentum Cooper pairing while leaving the critical current symmetry unchanged. The interplay between this linear term and the higher order gradient one leads to the Josephson diode effect.

</details>


### [88] [Topological superconductivity in an altermagnet-superconductor heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13408)
*Michael Liudeng,Hrishikesh Patel,Marcel Franz,Niclas Heinsdorf*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 提出将交替磁体单层V2Se2O与常规s波超导体复合，利用动量依赖交换劈裂把近邻诱导配对转化为全隙p波自旋三重态，并产生非零陈数与手性马约拉纳边缘模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全隙自旋三重态超导体是拓扑边缘输运和马约拉纳激发的理想平台；作者希望用实验可获得的交替磁半导体与常规超导体界面来实现该态。

Method: 从V2Se2O的第一性原理描述出发，构建其自旋劈裂费米面的低能模型，研究耦合异质结的超导不稳定性，计算Bogoliubov-de Gennes能带、陈数与slab谱，并量化反近邻效应及有限多层堆垛中的诱导能隙演化。

Result: 动量依赖的交换劈裂偏好等自旋配对，将近邻诱导超导序转变为全隙p波态；在较宽界面参数范围内，BdG能带具有非零陈数，slab谱显示穿过体能隙的手性马约拉纳边缘模。进一步表明诱导能隙会随有限多层堆垛变化。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了常规超导体与交替磁半导体界面实现拓扑三重态超导的路线，并为界面制备和实验探测提供实际指导。

Abstract: Fully gapped spin-triplet superconductors offer a natural setting for topological edge transport and Majorana excitations. Here we propose a heterostructure that interfaces altermagnetic monolayer V$_2$Se$_2$O with a conventional $s$-wave superconductor. Starting from an ab-initio description of V$_2$Se$_2$O, we construct a low-energy model of its spin-split Fermi surface and study the superconducting instability of the coupled stack. We find that the momentum-dependent exchange splitting of the altermagnet strongly favors equal-spin pairing and converts the proximity-induced order into a fully gapped $p$-wave state. Over a broad range of interface parameters, the resulting Bogoliubov-de Gennes bands carry a nonzero Chern number, and slab spectra exhibit chiral Majorana edge modes traversing the bulk gap. We further quantify the inverse proximity effect and show how the induced gap evolves across a finite multilayer stack. These results establish a route to topological triplet superconductivity by interfacing a conventional superconductor with an experimentally available altermagnetic semiconductor, and provide practical guidance for interface fabrication and experimental detection.

</details>


### [89] [Spatial instability of a homogeneous superconductor after an interaction quench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13426)
*Medha Roy Choudhury,Sankha Subhra Bakshi,Pinaki Majumdar,Amit Ghosal*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文研究吸引哈伯德模型描述的传统超导体在淬火哈伯德吸引后的非平衡量子动力学；通过全密度矩阵时间演化追踪配对场与密度场的时空行为，发现均匀动力学可失稳并出现空间织构，长时趋于空间非均匀性显著减弱的状态，且全局超导序参量显示两类淬火响应，其中一类伴随强非均匀配对与键电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解传统超导体远离平衡时，空间均匀动力学对淬火扰动的稳定性，以及淬火强度如何诱导自发空间结构、非均匀配对和键电流等非平衡超导响应。

Method: 以吸引哈伯德模型描述传统超导体，通过淬火哈伯德吸引强度将其驱动至远离平衡；采用海森堡运动方程演化系统全密度矩阵，追踪配对场和密度场的时空动力学；并施加极弱非均匀初始条件，以检验均匀动力学对空间微扰的稳定性。

Result: 淬火后初期，配对场和密度场在空间上保持均匀但随时间涨落；超过阈值时间后，取决于淬火强度，空间均匀动力学可失稳并出现空间织构。失稳起始时间依赖淬火强度和初始非均匀性。长时系统演化到空间非均匀性显著降低的状态。全局超导序参量的时间依赖表明存在两大类淬火响应，其中一类包含强非均匀配对和键电流。

Conclusion: 传统超导体的非平衡淬火动力学可因空间微扰从均匀态失稳，产生时空织构以及可能强非均匀的配对与键电流；最终趋于残余空间非均匀性较弱的状态。淬火强度调控两类响应，揭示非平衡超导中空间均匀性、序参量和电流结构之间的耦合。

Abstract: We investigate the quantum dynamics of a conventional superconductor, described by the attrac- tive Hubbard model, driven far from equilibrium by quenching the Hubbard attraction. We evolve the full density matrix of the system in time using the Heisenberg equation of motion, tracking the spatiotemporal dynamics of the pairing and density fields. We use very weakly inhomogeneous initial conditions to probe the stability of the homogeneous dynamics to spatial perturbations. At initial times after the quench the pairing and density fields remain homogeneous in space while fluctuating in time. However, depending on the quench strength the spatially uniform dynamics can become unstable beyond a threshold time, with emergence of spatial textures. The onset time for the instability depends on the strength of the quench and the initial inhomogeneity. At long times the system evolves towards a state with significantly reduced spatial heterogeneity. The time dependence of the global superconducting order parameter suggests two broad categories of re- sponse to a quench. One of these involves strongly inhomogeneous pairing and bond currents in the nonequilibrium superconductor.

</details>


### [90] [Disorder-driven transport in ZrN thin films grown by ion-beam-assisted sputtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13568)
*Maximo DiPreta,Samuel Jiang,Serhii Kruhlov,Yi Li,Valentine Novosad,Tomas Polakovic*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过离子束辅助溅射调控ZrN薄膜生长条件，可实现从金属超导到绝缘输运的连续转变，并揭示电子局域化与超导能标之间的解耦及非常规上临界场行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无序和生长条件如何调控ZrN薄膜的结构、电子输运与超导性质，并理解金属导电、电子局域化与超导之间的交叉机制。

Method: 采用直流离子束辅助溅射生长ZrN薄膜，系统改变氮分压、溅射功率和沉积温度；结合XRD结构表征、低温输运和磁输运测量，并用3D Mott变程跳跃、BCS模型、脏极限II型WHH模型及幂律进行拟合分析。
