Conclusion: IBAS生长的ZrN是一个可广泛调控的平台，可用于研究无序驱动输运以及金属导电、电子局域化和超导之间的交叉行为。

Abstract: We investigate the structural and electronic transport properties of zirconium nitride (ZrN) thin films grown by dc ion-beam assisted sputtering (IBAS) as a function of nitrogen partial pressure, sputtering power and deposition temperature. Variation of growth conditions enables controlled tuning from disordered metallic and superconducting behavior to insulating transport. Increasing nitrogen flow drives an increase in sheet resistance, suppression of superconducting transition temperature, and non-metallic conduction. XRD shows no measurable change in long range crystallinity or order. In the insulating regime, low-temperature transport is well described by 3D Mott variable-range hopping (VRH), with the characteristic temperature $T_0$ increasing monotonically with sheet resistance over four orders of magnitude. Despite this strong correlation between $T_0$ and disorder, $T_c$ exhibits no systematic dependence on $T_0$, indicating a decoupling between localization physics and the superconducting energy scale. Magnetotransport measurements are not well described by the standard BCS model nor the dirty type-II Werthamer-Helfand-Hohenberg model. The phase boundary is instead captured by a free-exponent power law, $\mu_0 H_{c2}(T) = \mu_0 H_{c2}(0)[1 - (T/T_c)^{n}]$ with $n \approx 3.47$, and extrapolates to $\mu_0 H_{c2}(0) \approx 6.4$ T. The films have an extracted coherence length of 7 nm. These results establish IBAS-grown ZrN as a broadly tunable platform for investigating disorder-driven transport and the crossover between metallic conduction, electronic localization, and superconductivity.

</details>


### [91] [Vortex rivers and multiple voltage transitions in superconducting MgB$_2$ thin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13882)
*Anton Pokusinskyi,Clemens Schmid,Thomas Hauet,Oleksandr Dobrovolskiy*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 基于含时金兹堡-朗道方程数值模拟不同无序超导薄膜的I-V曲线，发现缺陷形貌决定电压转变模式：晶界缺陷网络呈多重电压转变叠加非线性上翘，随机细长缺陷呈电压台阶，垂直电流的L形缺陷呈被电压转变分隔的扩展线性区；并分析序参量演化及实验可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合态第二类超导体的耗散和磁阻由涡旋动力学支配；大输运电流下I-V曲线常因磁通流不稳定性出现非线性上翘和突跳，但也可能形成相滑移线或正常态畴。主导机制取决于样品均匀性、尺寸相对相干长度与穿透深度、电子能量弛豫和排热速率，因此需厘清不同无序下的转变行为。

Method: 采用含时金兹堡-朗道方程，对具有不同无序类型的超导薄膜进行I-V曲线数值建模，包括晶界缺陷网络、随机排列的细长缺陷以及垂直于输运电流的L形缺陷；沿I-V曲线追踪序参量演化。

Result: 晶界缺陷网络导致I-V曲线出现多个电压转变并叠加非线性上翘；随机细长缺陷导致电压台阶；垂直电流的L形缺陷导致扩展线性区，并由电压转变分隔。不同无序形貌对应不同的涡旋/耗散转变特征。

Conclusion: 无序类型与缺陷取向可调控超导薄膜的I-V非线性、电压跃迁和线性区，揭示了除磁通流不稳定性外的多种动力学区制；序参量演化可解释这些行为，且相关区制具有实验可达性。

Abstract: Vortex dynamics govern the dissipation and magneto-resistive properties of type-II superconductors. At large transport currents, the current-voltage ($I$-$V$) characteristics of a superconductor in the mixed state usually exhibit a nonlinear upturn followed by an abrupt jump occurring due to a flux-flow instability. However, other transition behaviors are also possible, including the formation of phase-slip lines and normal domains. Which mechanism dominates depends on the sample uniformity, its dimensions relative to the coherence length and penetration depth, and the rates of electron energy relaxation and heat removal. Here, based on the time-dependent Ginzburg-Landau equation, we present the results of numerical modeling of the $I$-$V$ curves of superconducting films with various types of disorder. For a grain-boundary defect mesh, we find multiple voltage transitions overlaid with a nonlinear upturn of the $I$-$V$ curves. For randomly arranged elongated defects, the $I$-$V$ curves exhibit voltage steps, whereas for L-shaped defects oriented perpendicular to the transport current, the $I$-$V$ curves show extended linear regimes separated by voltage transitions. We analyze the evolution of the order parameter along the $I$-$V$ curves and discuss the experimental accessibility of the revealed dynamics regimes.

</details>


### [92] [Altermagnetism across the BCS-BEC crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14613)
*Iogann Tolbatov,Luca Salasnich*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 研究二维配对费米系统中交错磁性引起的动量依赖自旋劈裂，在零温下发现完全能隙的 BCS-BEC 跨域中相位刚度普遍满足二次抑制关系 J/J0=1-(α/α0)^2；BCS 侧出现无隙 Bogoliubov 口袋后偏离该关系并可能诱导有限动量配对，BEC 侧则对应紧束缚复合玻色子有效质量的改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解交错磁性产生的动量依赖自旋劈裂如何破坏费米子配对，并系统比较其在 BCS 与 BEC 两侧对配对稳定性、相位刚度和有限动量配对倾向的影响。

Method: 采用二维配对费米系统，以双体束缚能描述相互作用强度；在固定密度下自洽求解化学势与配对能隙，使其从弱耦合 BCS 区演化到强耦合 BEC 区；通过赋予配对对的质心动量并计算自由能变化来检验均匀配对态的稳定性。

Result: 零温下，在整个完全能隙的 BCS-BEC 跨域中相位刚度呈现普适二次抑制 J/J0=1-(α/α0)^2；偏离仅出现在 BCS 侧，当无隙 Bogoliubov 口袋打开时刚度迅速降低，并可触发向有限动量配对的失稳；在 BEC 区，该普适二次修正对应紧束缚复合玻色子有效质量的改变。

Conclusion: 该模型为比较交错磁性对 BCS 与 BEC 跨域两侧配对破坏效应提供了一个简洁框架。

Abstract: We study a two-dimensional paired Fermi system in which altermagnetism produces a spin splitting that depends on momentum. The interaction strength is described through the two-body binding energy, so that the chemical potential and pairing gap evolve self-consistently from the weak-coupling Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) regime to the strong-coupling Bose-Einstein-condensate (BEC) regime at fixed density. The stability of the uniform paired state is examined by giving the pairs a small center-of-mass momentum and following the resulting change in free energy. We show that at zero temperature, the phase stiffness follows a universal quadratic suppression, $J/J_0 = 1 - (\alpha/\alpha_0)^2$, across the entire fully gapped crossover regime. Deviations from this relation emerge only on the BCS side upon the opening of gapless Bogoliubov pockets, which rapidly reduce the stiffness and can trigger an instability toward finite-momentum pairing. In the BEC regime, this universal quadratic correction corresponds to the altered effective mass of the tightly bound composite bosons. The model therefore provides a simple setting in which to compare altermagnetic pair breaking on the BCS and BEC sides of the crossover.

</details>


### [93] [Growth of superconducting boron doped diamond on 4inch silicon wafers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15282)
*Soumen Mandal,Oliver A Williams*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 采用MPCVD在4英寸Si晶圆上生长超导硼掺杂金刚石薄膜，发现Tc随气相B/C比先升后降，最高为4.03 K，并揭示大面积生长时硼掺入效率降低和径向不均匀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现晶圆级大面积超导硼掺杂金刚石薄膜，并理解气相硼浓度对硼掺入、表面形貌和超导性能的影响，为4英寸晶圆尺度优化提供依据。

Method: 采用微波等离子体化学气相沉积，在4英寸Si晶圆上以6536至36421 ppm气相B/C比生长BDD薄膜；通过表面形貌、Raman光谱、电阻-温度测量和上临界场测量，表征硼掺入、超导转变及空间均匀性，并与2英寸晶圆生长结果比较。

Result: 表观横向晶粒尺寸无系统变化；除6536 ppm样品在2 K以上未超导外，其余样品均超导。Tc随B/C比升高先增加，在24691 ppm达到最高4.03 K，随后降低；2 K下电阻上临界场达3.091 T。Raman显示硼掺入随B/C比增加至30303 ppm，在36421 ppm略降。24691 ppm薄膜中心、中间和边缘位置Tc分别为4.02、4.19和3.33 K，Raman显示相应硼浓度空间变化。与2英寸生长相比，4英寸晶圆需更高气相B/C比才能获得相当硼浓度和超导性能，表明大面积生长中硼掺入效率降低。

Conclusion: 证明在4英寸Si晶圆较大面积上制备超导BDD可行；硼掺入效率和径向均匀性是进一步晶圆级优化的关键参数。

Abstract: Superconducting boron-doped diamond (BDD) films were grown on 4-inch silicon wafers by microwave plasma chemical vapour deposition using gas-phase B/C ratios ranging from 6536 to 36421 ppm. Surface morphology, boron incorporation and superconducting properties were investigated as a function of gas-phase boron concentration. No systematic variation in apparent lateral grain size was observed across the series. Superconductivity was observed in all films except that grown at a B/C ratio of 6536 ppm within the measured temperature range down to 2 K. The superconducting transition temperature initially increased with increasing B/C ratio, reaching a maximum T$_c$ of 4.03 K at 24691 ppm, before decreasing at higher gas-phase B/C ratios. The corresponding resistive upper critical field at 2 K reached 3.091 T. Raman spectroscopy showed an increase in boron incorporation with increasing gas-phase B/C ratio up to 30303 ppm, followed by a slight decrease at 36421 ppm. Spatial measurements across the film grown at 24691 ppm showed T$_c$ values of 4.02, 4.19 and 3.33 K at the centre, intermediate and edge positions, respectively, with Raman spectroscopy showing a corresponding spatial variation in boron concentration. Comparison with previous growth on 2-inch wafers showed that substantially higher gas-phase B/C ratios were required to obtain comparable boron concentrations and superconducting properties on 4-inch wafers, indicating reduced boron incorporation efficiency during large-area growth. These results demonstrate the feasibility of producing superconducting BDD over a substantial area of a 4-inch silicon wafer while identifying boron incorporation and radial uniformity as key parameters for further wafer-scale optimisation.

</details>


### [94] [Multigap superconductivity in Ising superconductors: The case of (LaSe)1.14(NbSe2)m misfit layer compounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15446)
*Alexandra Palacio-Morales,Tomas Samuely,Ludovica Zullo,Raphaël T. Leriche,Pavol Szabó,Shunsuke Sasaki,Hugo Le Du,Christophe Brun,François Debontridder,Giovanni Marini,Marek Kuzmiak,Jozef Kačmarčík,Laurent Cario,Matteo Calandra,Tristan Cren,Peter Samuely*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过方向性扫描隧道谱与各向异性Migdal-Eliashberg计算，发现NbSe2基层状错配化合物中存在各向异性多能隙超导态：Γ口袋能隙脆弱，K/K'口袋能隙稳健，并暗示非传统甚至可能拓扑超导配对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单层NbSe2中的Ising超导及块体错配化合物中可能实现非常规/拓扑超导的提议，促使研究者探究由交替TMD与岩盐层构成、并嵌入三维晶体中高度掺杂且电子解耦NbSe2片的错配化合物的超导序参量。

Method: 对错配超导体(LaSe)1.14(NbSe2)和(LaSe)1.14(NbSe2)2进行方向性扫描隧道谱测量，并与各向异性Migdal-Eliashberg计算得到的动量分辨能隙Δ(k)进行对比。

Result: 观察到强各向异性多能隙超导态：Γ中心费米面口袋上的脆弱能隙与K、K'口袋上稳健的本征能隙共存；结果与各向异性Migdal-Eliashberg计算的Δ(k)定量一致。Γ中心能隙的显著脆弱性以及临界温度对非磁性无序的强敏感性表明配对超出常规s波对称性，可能涉及拓扑序参量。

Conclusion: NbSe2基错配化合物被确立为可调块体平台，用于研究多能隙非常规超导；Ising保护为通向拓扑配对提供了有前景的途径。

Abstract: Strong spin-orbit coupling and broken inversion symmetry in transition metal dichalcogenides give rise to Ising superconductivity, a spin-protected pairing state first identified in monolayer NbSe$_2$ through in-plane critical fields far exceeding the Pauli limit. More recently, Ising superconductivity has been proposed as a potential route to unconventional and even topological superconductivity in bulk misfit compounds. Here, we investigate the superconducting order parameter of layered misfit compounds composed of alternating transition metal dichalcogenide and rocksalt layers, which host extremely doped, electronically decoupled NbSe$_2$ sheets within a three-dimensional crystal. Using directional scanning tunneling spectroscopy on the misfit superconductors (LaSe)$_{1.14}$(NbSe$_2$) and (LaSe)$_{1.14}$(NbSe$_2$)$_2$, we uncover a strongly anisotropic multigap superconducting state: a fragile gap on the $\Gamma$-centered Fermi-surface pocket coexists with a robust, intrinsic gap on the K and K$'$ pockets. These features are in quantitative agreement with momentum-resolved gaps $\Delta(\mathbf{k})$ obtained from anisotropic Migdal-Eliashberg calculations. The marked fragility of the $\Gamma$-centered gap, combined with the strong sensitivity of the critical temperature to non-magnetic disorder, points to pairing beyond conventional $s$-wave symmetry, potentially involving a topological order parameter. These results establish NbSe$_2$-based misfit compounds as a tunable bulk platform for multigap, unconventional superconductivity, with Ising protection offering a promising route toward topological pairing.

</details>


### [95] [Thermal Hall effect in elemental niobium, a two-gap superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15663)
*Jing Zhang,Xiaokang Li,Kamran Behnia,Zengwei Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过热霍尔效应测量，在铌（Nb）超导转变温度以下发现横向热输运主导载流子由空穴型转变为电子型，直接证明其双能隙超导性，并拟合出约0.22 k_B T_c的次要能隙。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铌是超导史上关键元素，也是长期被怀疑具有多带配对的超导体；自1959年提出双能隙图像以来，实验证据始终存在争议且难以确认。

Method: 采用热霍尔效应测量，并结合纵向热导率分析；将数据拟合到双能隙Bardeen-Rickayzen-Tewordt（BRT）模型。

Result: 在T_c以下观察到横向热输运交叉，热霍尔系数发生符号反转，主导载流子从空穴型变为电子型；拟合得到第二个能隙约0.22 k_B T_c，仅为主能隙的11%。电子型纵向热导贡献开始占主导的温度与热霍尔系数符号反转起始温度一致，并可用于将超导能隙分配给空穴型或电子型费米面片。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了铌双能隙超导的长期争议，表明多能隙超导比此前认为的更普遍，并证明热霍尔效应是分辨超导体能隙多重性的有力探针。

Abstract: Niobium holds a pivotal place in superconductivity history: it is not only the elemental superconductor with the highest critical temperature, but also a long-standing candidate for multiband pairing whose evidence has remained controversial. A two-gap scenario was proposed as early as 1959, yet experimental proof stayed elusive. Here, through thermal Hall effect measurements, we unambiguously demonstrate a crossover in transverse thermal transport below $T_{\rm c}$, where the dominant carrier switches from hole-like to electron-like upon cooling. This crossover is a clear hallmark of two distinct superconducting condensates. Fitting our data to a two-gap Bardeen-Rickayzen-Tewordt (BRT) model yields a second energy gap of approximately 0.22 $k_{\rm B}T_{\rm c}$, only 11\% of the dominant gap. This small gap size accounts for the difficulty in resolving the two-gap structure in earlier experiments. Crucially, the temperature at which the electron-like contribution to longitudinal thermal conductivity begins to dominate coincides with the onset of the sign reversal in the thermal Hall coefficient, confirming consistency between the two methods, and providing crucial information on the assignment of the superconducting gaps to their respective hole- or electron-like Fermi surface sheets. These findings not only resolve a longstanding controversy, but also establish that multigap superconductivity is far more common than previously assumed, and demonstrate the thermal Hall effect as a powerful probe for resolving gap multiplicities in superconductors.

</details>


### [96] [Wrinkles and Magnetic Flux Trapping in Graphite Nanoflakes: A Possible Source and Manifestation of Room-Temperature Superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15712)
*Muhammad Saad,Sergey I. Nikitin,Dmitry A. Tayurskii,Roman V. Yusupov*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过研磨块状热解石墨并在空气中670 K退火，可在石墨纳米片中产生高密度皱褶缺陷，并观察到高达390 K以上的磁通俘获，暗示皱褶区域是局域高温超导的可能来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究石墨中是否存在局域高温超导性，以及缺陷/微观结构变化是否与其出现相关。

Method: 将块状热解石墨长时间研磨，并在空气中670 K退火；用TEM表征基面表面缺陷，并测量磁通俘获信号；同时对比未退火研磨样品和真空退火样品作为对照。

Result: 空气退火样品TEM显示基面形成高密度皱褶型缺陷，并出现磁通俘获，且可保持到390 K及以上；未退火和真空退火样品磁通俘获可忽略，TEM中也未见皱褶。

Conclusion: 高密度皱褶阵列的出现与室温以上磁通俘获之间存在直接关联，表明皱褶区域可能是石墨中局域高温超导性的来源。

Abstract: The paper reports on a possible manifestation of local high-temperature superconductivity in graphite nanoflakes obtained from the bulk pyrolytic graphite by an extended grinding and subsequent annealing at 670 K in the air. Analysis of transmission electron microscopy (TEM) images shows that such treatment leads to a formation of high-density wrinkle-type defects at the basal-plane surface. The sample reveals the magnetic flux trapping - one of the key signatures of superconductivity - that persists up to 390 K and above. At the same time, both the as-ground (unannealed) and annealed in the vacuum samples manifest negligible flux trapping, and no wrinkles in their TEM images were found. The direct correlation between the appearance of high-density wrinkle arrays and the detection of the trapped magnetic flux above the room temperature strongly suggests that wrinkled regions serve a source of local high-temperature superconductivity in graphite.

</details>


### [97] [Stripe-Like Superconducting Enhancement and Coexisting Magnetic Texture in an Infinite Layer Nickelate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15958)
*Ryan Laing,Dung Vu,Jacob Pfund,Wenzheng Wei,Frederick J. Walker,Haiyan Tan,Pavel Volkov,Menka Jain,Charles Ahn,Ilya Sochnikov*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用扫描SQUID显微镜研究最优掺杂NENO薄膜，发现超导与弱磁性织构共存及条带状超导增强，表明竞争磁相导致超导转变展宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 镍酸盐薄膜作为铜酸盐的结构与电子类似物很有前景，但其超导转变普遍展宽且机制不清；局域测量有望揭示缺陷或竞争相引起的相分离。

Method: 采用扫描SQUID显微镜对分子束外延生长的最优掺杂Nd1-xEuxNiO2（x=0.25）薄膜进行局域超流密度和磁结构成像，并进行空间分辨磁化率测量。

Result: 观测到与超导共存的弱磁性织构；空间分辨磁化率成像显示超导态高度非均匀，在相变附近出现稳健的条带状增强图案。

Conclusion: 结果揭示介观尺度电子与磁不均匀性，表明竞争磁相是这些最优掺杂NENO样品超导转变异常展宽的主要原因；理解该超导增强机制可能为提升镍酸盐超导温度提供途径。

Abstract: Despite their promise as structural and electronic analogs to cuprates, nickelate thin films consistently exhibit broadened superconducting transitions in many experiments that remain poorly understood. Local measurements are ideal for revealing the presence of defects or competing phases, which may cause this transition broadening through a phase-separation. Here, we use scanning SQUID microscopy to investigate local superfluid density and magnetic texture of optimally doped $\mathrm{Nd}_{1-x}\mathrm{Eu}_{x}\mathrm{NiO}_{2}$ (NENO) (x=0.25) grown via molecular beam epitaxy. We observed a weakly-magnetic texture coexisting with superconductivity. Spatially resolved susceptibility imaging reveals a highly non-uniform superconducting state, characterized by a robust stripe-like enhancement pattern appearing near the phase transition. By resolving the mesoscopic landscape of electronic and magnetic inhomogeneities, these findings suggest that a competing magnetic phase is a primary contributor to the unusually broad superconducting transitions of these optimally doped NENO samples. Understanding the uncovered superconducting enhancement may provide a path to raising superconducting temperatures in these materials.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [Topological classification through knotted graphs: Fermi surface dispersions and Lifshitz transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13390)
*Hakan Akgün,Xianquan Yan,Ching Hua Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出以扭结图（knotted graphs）统一描述能带色散与费米面拓扑，并引入 Yamada 集/序列框架，超越传统 Yamada 多项式，可完整刻画真实材料费米面及其嵌套结构，并连接 Lifshitz 转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纽结不变量仅能分类带隙闭合处的 1D 节点线，无法编码真实材料的完整色散和复杂费米面结构，因此需要新的拓扑语言。

Method: 采用 knotted graphs 作为能带拓扑分类框架；提出边界分辨的 Yamada 集与随能量演化的 Yamada 序列，以完整刻画费米面及多个不连通部分的嵌套；用 DFT 能带结构验证，并推广至非厄米奇异面，结合 Berry 曲率通量赋予扭结图骨架有向 Abelian 边流。

Result: 该框架可统一分类整个能带色散，部分情形下也涵盖本征态拓扑；Yamada 序列作为紧凑的色散级指纹，与 Lifshitz 转变的实验特征直接关联；在真实材料 DFT 能带和非厄米奇异面中展示适用性。

Conclusion: knotted graphs 与 Yamada 集/序列为真实材料能带和费米面拓扑提供了统一、可实验关联的分类框架，并可扩展至非厄米体系以刻画本征态拓扑。

Abstract: Knot theory has provided a rich topological taxonomy for band structures, but its reach is fundamentally limited: knot invariants classify only 1D nodal lines at gap closure, and cannot encode the full dispersion or rich Fermi surface structure of realistic materials. Here we show that knotted graphs (knots that admit graph-like intersections in 3D space) - which have so far been elusive in condensed matter literature - provide a unified topological language for classifying the entire band dispersion, and even the eigenstate topology in some contexts. We propose a new framework beyond the existing Yamada polynomials that can topologically characterize the intricacies of realistic Fermi surfaces completely, crucially including how their multiple disconnected pieces are nested. This yields the Yamada set, a boundary-resolved extension which organizes the full topological evolution across energy into a Yamada sequence: a compact dispersion-level fingerprint directly tied to experimental signatures of Lifshitz transitions. Our framework is demonstrated with DFT-based band structures of real materials. Beyond dispersion-level classifications, this framework can be extended to non-Hermitian exceptional surfaces, where Berry-curvature flux further equips the knotted-graph skeleton with a directed Abelian edge flow that also captures the eigenstate topology.

</details>


### [99] [Effect of Buried-Interface Preparation for Nb Superconducting Resonators on InP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13673)
*Logan S. Kusher,Ding Peng,Zihua Zhu,Arunav Bordoloi,Axel Leblanc,Lukas J. Baker,Nichae Adnan,Jacob Issokson,Alvin Wang,Frederik Knudsen,Krishna Dindial,Melissa Mikalsen,Taha Kaleem,Andrei Vrajitoarea,Yingge Du,Patrick J. Strohbeen,Javad Shabani*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究比较 InP 衬底三种表面处理对溅射 Nb 薄膜及其超导微波谐振器性能的影响，发现 S 钝化改善界面与超导性，但微波品质因子并未相应提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: III-V 器件中去除 native oxide 很关键；但在面向量子信息的超导-半导体混合体系中，衬底处理对界面与器件性能的影响尚不明确。

Method: 在 InP 上沉积溅射 Nb，比较三种前处理：无意去氧化层 control、原位 Ar+ 铣削、S 钝化；表征金属-衬底界面 O 浓度、表面/埋入界面粗糙度、Nb 结构、超导转变温度及微波谐振响应。

Result: Ar+ 铣削降低金属-衬底界面 O，但粗化 InP、增厚有效 Nb-InP 界面并促 O 经 Nb 缺陷掺入；S 钝化更有效抑制界面 O，保持更尖锐光滑的埋入界面，并获更高 Tc 和更少结构损伤。三者的微波响应相近；单光子下最高 Qi 分别约为 control 1.30e5、S 钝化 9.7e4、Ar+ 铣削 8.4e4。

Conclusion: 当前器件的微波损耗并非主要受埋入 Nb-InP 界面介电损耗限制；埋入界面的材料优化未必直接转化为微波性能提升。

Abstract: Substrate surface preparation is a key step in the fabrication of electronic devices. In III--V semiconductor platforms, e.g. used in HEMTs and lasers, removal of the native substrate oxide is extremely important, where improper removal will negatively affect end-of-line device performance. However, the impact of substrate preparation in hybrid superconductor--semiconductor (S--Sm) systems relevant to quantum information applications remains poorly understood. This study compares three surface preparations for the deposition of sputtered Nb films on InP: (i) no intentional oxide removal (control), (ii) \textit{in-situ} $\mathrm{Ar}^{+}$ milling, and (iii) S-passivation. \textit{In-situ} $\mathrm{Ar}^{+}$ milling reduces the O concentration at the metal--substrate (MS) interface, but also roughens the InP surface, increasing the effective thickness of the Nb--InP interface and promoting O incorporation through extended defects in the Nb film. S-passivation suppresses interfacial O more effectively while preserving a sharper and smoother buried interface. It also yields Nb films with higher superconducting transition temperatures and less structural damage than the $\mathrm{Ar}^{+}$-milled samples. Despite these materials improvements, the microwave response is comparable across the three preparations. At single photon powers, the highest internal quality factors, $Q_i$, are approximately $1.30\times10^{5}$ ($130\mathrm{k}$) for the control resonators, $9.7\times10^{4}$ ($97\mathrm{k}$) for the S-passivated resonators, and $8.4\times10^{4}$ ($84\mathrm{k}$) for the $\mathrm{Ar}^{+}$-milled resonators. These results suggest that the present devices are not primarily limited by dielectric loss at the buried Nb--InP interface.

</details>


### [100] [Heating of black-Si by microwaves at 2.45 GHz: effect of nano-needle orientation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13683)
*Massimiliano Zamengo,Haoran Mu,Hsin-Hui Huang,Tomas Katkus,Darius Gailevičius,Saulius Juodkazis,Junko Morikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过SF6/O2等离子体刻蚀制备的黑硅纳米针结构可在2.45 GHz微波下显著升温；垂直纳米针加热最强，倾斜纳米针表现出延迟但突变的加热行为，并伴随谐振频移和Q变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅在2.45 GHz微波段高反射且几乎不吸收，难以直接微波加热；研究旨在利用黑硅纳米针表面纹理增强微波吸收/加热，并比较垂直与倾斜纳米针的各向异性响应。

Method: 在相同条件下用圆柱形TM010谐振腔比较平面硅、垂直纳米针黑硅和倾斜（取向）纳米针黑硅的微波加热。样品水平置于150 μm厚盖玻片上以固定高度和位置，并用K型热电偶校准辐射温度计。采用2 W、3 W、4 W分步微波功率，每步60 s，同时原位监测腔谐振频率f和品质因数Q。

Result: 垂直纳米针黑硅达到约260 °C，高于平面硅约190 °C；倾斜纳米针黑硅在3 W时出现延迟但突然的升温。所有样品均出现2–3 MHz的谐振频率下移，且下移在温度超过约120–150 °C后出现，符合硅中热激活载流子产生。相同温度下各样品对腔的负载对应不同f和Q，表明纳米针阵列的有效介电常数具有取向依赖性，与倾斜黑硅的强形状双折射一致。

Conclusion: 黑硅纳米针可显著改善硅的微波加热性能，其垂直/倾斜取向导致不同加热动力学和微波介电响应；该策略具应用潜力，但定量确定温度与有效介电常数仍面临挑战。

Abstract: Silicon, which is highly reflective and has negligible absorption at the microwave 2.45 GHz range, can be heated when it is turned into black-Si with a surface texture of nano-needles made by SF6/O2 plasma etching. Microwave heating of flat Si and of black-Si with vertical and with tilted (oriented) nano-needles was compared under identical conditions in a cylindrical TM010 resonator. Samples were placed horizontally on a 150-micrometer-thick cover glass, which standardized their height and position inside the cavity and allowed a common calibration of the radiation thermometer against a K-type thermocouple. Under a stepwise protocol of only 2-3-4 W of microwave power (60 s per step), black-Si with vertical needles reached ~ 260 C, systematically exceeding flat Si (~ 190 C), while black-Si with oriented needles showed a delayed but abrupt onset of heating at 3 W. In situ monitoring of the cavity resonance revealed a 2-3 MHz downshift of the resonance frequency f for all samples, with the drift consistently appearing once the sample temperature exceeded ~ 120 - 150 C, consistent with thermally activated carrier generation in Si. At a given temperature, each sample loads the cavity at a different f and quality factor Q, indicating an orientation-dependent effective permittivity of the nano-needle array, in line with the strong form birefringence of tilted black-Si. Application potential and challenges in the quantitative temperature and permittivity determination are discussed.

</details>


### [101] [The effect of light scattering in cavity electrodynamics: Fresnel equations with decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14241)
*Natalya A. Zimbovskaya,Abraham Nitzan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究Fabry-Pérot微腔中光退相干对其线性光学响应的影响；退相干会逐渐侵蚀腔光子模，抑制分子极化激元形成，并使透射与吸收谱中的极化激元特征减弱直至消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究光退相干如何改变微腔对入射光的线性响应，以及它如何影响腔光子模和分子极化激元的形成。

Method: 用经典电动力学描述腔镜的光学性质；将引起相位破坏的光散射作为多通道散射问题处理，并采用Büttiker用于电子输运退相干的模型。

Result: 退相干导致腔光子模逐步被侵蚀；随着散射增强，极化激元在微腔透射和吸收光谱中的特征逐渐减弱并消失。

Conclusion: 光退相干会显著改变Fabry-Pérot微腔的线性响应，并阻碍分子极化激元的形成，从而抹除其光谱特征。

Abstract: We consider the effect of light decoherence in a Fabri-Perot (FP) microcavity. We show that, when present, it may significantly change its linear response to the incident light.The optical properties of the bounding mirrors are described using the classical electrodynamics, and the light scattering causing the phase breaking is treated as a multichannel scattering problem employing a model introduced by B\"uttiker to describe dephasing in electron transport. We show that the dephasing causes a gradual erosion of the cavity photon mode and thus impedes the formation of molecular polaritons.
  Consequently, the polaritons signatures in the transmission and absorption optical spectra of the microcavity are fading away as the scattering intensifies.

</details>


### [102] [Spectral density of angular momentum transfer from a swift electron to a large spherical nanoparticle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14319)
*Jorge Luis Briseño-Gómez,Alejandro Reyes-Coronado*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种完全延迟、因果且多极收敛的电动力学方法，用于计算快速电子向孤立球形纳米颗粒的角动量转移，并以铝和金纳米颗粒为例解析其全频域频谱特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论要么采用仅适用于远小于实验相关尺寸颗粒的偶极近似，要么仅给出频率积分后的多极结果而无法分辨相互作用的频谱结构，因此需要一种可处理实验相关大尺寸、光学复杂材料且计算可行的多极方法。

Method: 基于闭合曲面Maxwell应力张量，将角向积分解析化为与材料及电子轨迹无关、由关联Legendre函数构成的小型不可约积分集合，实现完全延迟、因果、多极收敛计算；将双多极求和的复杂度从O(l_max^4)降至O(l_max^3)，使a=50 nm颗粒可收敛至l_max=51，成本降低三至四个数量级。

Result: 应用于铝和金纳米颗粒（至a=50 nm）后，获得全频域角动量转移谱密度；转移由电子场与散射场干涉决定，几乎在所有频率占主导；电贡献比磁贡献高两至三个数量级，频谱分辨还揭示磁项存在被频率积分掩盖的符号变化。在a=50 nm、距颗粒中心b=51 nm时，金比铝转移更多角动量，且随速度增大，从v=0.5c约2倍增至v→c时超过6倍。

Conclusion: 该框架克服了偶极近似的尺寸限制和频率积分多极计算缺乏频谱信息的不足，为电子束驱动纳米尺度角动量操控（电子镊子）提供了高效且可收敛至实验相关大颗粒的预测工具。

Abstract: Swift electrons in scanning transmission electron microscopy transfer both linear and angular momentum to nanoparticles, underlying electron-beam-driven nanoscale manipulation ("electron tweezers"). Prior theory relied either on the small-particle (dipolar) approximation, valid only well below experimentally relevant sizes, or on frequency-integrated multipolar calculations that leave the spectral structure of the interaction unresolved. Here we present a fully retarded, causal, multipole-converged electrodynamical methodology for the angular momentum transfer from a swift electron to an isolated spherical nanoparticle, based on a closed-surface Maxwell stress tensor formulation whose angular integrals reduce analytically to a small, material- and trajectory-independent set of irreducible integrals over associated Legendre functions. This lowers the cost of the double multipolar sum from O(l_max^4) to O(l_max^3), enabling convergence up to l_max=51 for nanoparticles as large as a=50 nm, nearly four times the order of the largest previous calculation at this size and previously unreached for an optically complex material, at three to four orders of magnitude lower cost. Applied to aluminum and gold nanoparticles up to a=50 nm, the method resolves the transfer's spectral density across the full frequency domain, showing it is set by interference between the electron and scattered fields, dominating at essentially every frequency; the electric contribution exceeds the magnetic one by two to three orders of magnitude, though spectral resolution reveals sign changes in the magnetic term hidden in the frequency-integrated transfer. At a=50 nm, gold transfers substantially more angular momentum than aluminum despite its more intricate response, by a factor growing with velocity from about 2x at v=0.5c to more than 6x as v->c (fixed b=51 nm from the nanoparticle center).

</details>


### [103] [First Demonstration of Flip DRAM from Process, Architecture to System to Push DRAM Scaling beyond 4F2: 2F2 Self-aligned Flip Vertical Channel Transistor (FVCT) DRAM and Flip WL (FWL) 3D-DRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14464)
*Yu Liu,Xinyue He,Yanbang Chu,Siyuan Liu,Jianxiang Jin,Fangcheng Sun,Yuyang Qiao,Xu Tian,Lan Li,Baokang Peng,Lining Zhang,Xing Wu,Pengpeng Ren,Zhigang Ji,Zongwei Wang,Lijie Zhang,Xinwei Wan,Weihai Bu,Ming Li,Runsheng Wang,Heng Wu,Ru Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 首次提出一种基于晶圆翻转与背面工艺的DRAM堆叠技术，充分利用DRAM晶圆背面，并在4F²与3D-DRAM上验证；其中2F² Flip VCT和3D flip WL staircase均显著改善寄生、速度、面积与密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为持续推进DRAM微缩，传统4F² VCT等方案在面积、寄生、感测裕度和工艺集成方面面临限制，需要利用晶圆背面与堆叠工艺开辟新的scaling路径。

Method: 提出翻转/背面堆叠技术：在4F² VCT中开发2F² Flip VCT，采用自对准背靠背堆叠1T1C bitcell及多种BL/WL配置；开发自对准堆叠垂直沟道、BL/WL形成、晶圆键合与翻转、衬底减薄、低阻Co存储节点等关键模块，并建立从器件到mat与chip级的DRAM DTCO框架。在3D-DRAM中提出新型翻转WL staircase设计及外围电路创新。

Result: 相同mat尺寸下，2F² FVCT较4F² VCT DRAM寄生降低27.5%，感测裕度提升11%，电荷共享速度提升16.3%，面积减少50%。3D-DRAM翻转WL staircase方案实现25%密度增益、15.1%更快开启速度、6.8%更少电荷共享时间。

Conclusion: 翻转与背面堆叠技术可有效缓解热、对准和寄生问题，并在4F²与3D-DRAM中展现可扩展性，为DRAM持续微缩提供了有前景的新方向。

Abstract: For the first time, we proposed a novel stacking technology for DRAM scaling by flipping and backside processes, making full use of DRAM wafer's backside and investigating it on both 4F2 and 3D-DRAM. For 4F2 VCT, 2F2 Flip VCT featuring self-aligned back-to-back stacked 1T1C bitcell, with various BL and WL configurations, were studied and key process modules such as self-aligned stacked vertical channel, BL and WL formations, wafer bonding and flipping, substrate thinning and low-R Co storage node (SN) were successfully developed, addressing the potential thermal, misalign and parasitic concerns in the Flip VCT process. A full DRAM DTCO framework was also established from device to mat and chip level. Compared to 4F2 VCT DRAM with the same mat size, 2F2 FVCT delivers 27.5% less parasitics, 11% better sense margin, 16.3% higher charge sharing (CS) speed and 50% less area. For 3D-DRAM, a brand-new flip WL staircase design with peripheral circuit innovations was studied and proved to have 25% density gain, 15.1% faster turn-on speed and 6.8% less CS time, proving further extendibility of flip technology on DRAM.

</details>


### [104] [Giant second-harmonic generation in few-atomic layer metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14468)
*Alessio Zaccone*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在三维量子限域理论中，金属薄膜的费米能级态密度增强有限（上限约10.1%），但二阶非线性极化率中的双共振分母可显著放大电子相空间重分布，从而解释Ag(111)中厚度归一化SHG效率约100倍增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子限域会改变超薄金属的电子相空间，但通常费米能级态密度变化并不大；作者旨在寻找并解释限域下二阶非线性光学响应可被大幅增强的机制。

Method: 基于抑制长波长电子态的三维限域理论，分析弱限域支的费米能级态密度上限；进一步用二阶非线性极化率中的共振分母和最小双共振闭合模型，预测薄膜厚度趋近 L_c=(2π/n)^{1/3}（n为载流子密度）且主导光跃迁路径处于退相干线宽内的共振条件。

Result: 理论给出费米能级态密度增强上限为 (4/3)^{1/3}-1≈10.1%；在双共振条件下，拟合Ag(111)实验得到有效相空间载流子密度 n_eff≈3.6×10^20 cm^-3，|χ^(2)|增强约10倍，从而产生厚度归一化SHG效率约100倍增加，并再现非振荡厚度依赖。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，限域金属的非线性光学增强主要来自二阶非线性中的共振相空间重分布，而非单纯的费米能级态密度增加；双共振闭合为解释和设计超薄金属SHG提供定量框架。

Abstract: Quantum confinement restructures the electronic phase space of ultrathin metals, but the resulting density-of-states (DOS) change is not generically large. We show that, within a three-dimensional confinement theory based on the suppression of long-wavelength electronic states, the Fermi-level DOS enhancement on the weak-confinement branch is bounded by $(4/3)^{1/3}-1\simeq10.1\%$. Instead, confinement-induced redistribution of electronic phase space can be strongly amplified by resonant denominators in the second-order susceptibility. A minimal double-resonance closure predicts giant amplification when the film thickness approaches $L_c=(2\pi/n)^{1/3}$, where $n$ is the carrier density, and a dominant optical pathway lies within a dephasing linewidth of resonance. Applied to recent Ag(111) measurements, the theory gives an effective phase-space carrier density parameter $n_{\rm eff}\simeq3.6\times10^{20}\,\mathrm{cm}^{-3}$, a tenfold enhancement of $|\chi^{(2)}|$, and hence the observed $\sim100$-fold increase of thickness-normalized second-harmonic-generation efficiency, together with the observed non-oscillatory thickness dependence.

</details>


### [105] [Persistent nonlinear Hall effect driven by parallel field across a topological phase transition and intraband sign-reversing integer quantum Hall effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14683)
*Suheel Ahmad Malik,M. A. H. Ahsan,SK Firoz Islam*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究了二维Rashba自旋轨道耦合节线环系统中的线性和非线性霍尔效应，发现垂直磁场可导致量子反常/量子霍尔效应及Landau能级斜率变号，平行磁场可诱导Berry曲率各向异性产生非线性霍尔效应，并可作为拓扑相变探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Rashba自旋轨道耦合与节线环拓扑电子态中的线性/非线性霍尔响应，以及磁场调控下拓扑相变与非线性霍尔效应之间的关系。

Method: 基于二维Rashba自旋轨道耦合节线环模型，计算Berry曲率、反常霍尔效应、垂直磁场下的整数量子霍尔效应与精确Landau能级；进一步施加平行磁场，分析其对Berry曲率各向异性及非线性霍尔电导的影响。

Result: 沿节线环边界出现依赖于节线环半径和Rashba强度的拓扑能隙，产生量子反常霍尔效应；垂直磁场下Landau能级斜率在环边界附近随磁场变号，导致同一能带量子霍尔电导变号；平行磁场诱导Berry曲率各向异性并产生非线性霍尔效应，且可闭合/重开拓扑能隙，实现从拓扑Chern绝缘体到平庸绝缘体的反向拓扑相变；非线性霍尔在两相均存在，但在Chern相随化学势呈变号峰结构，在平庸相呈单符号响应。

Conclusion: 非线性霍尔效应可作为探测Rashba自旋轨道耦合节线环系统中磁场驱动拓扑相变的有效探针。

Abstract: We investigate the linear and nonlinear Hall effects in a two-dimensional Rashba spin-orbit coupled nodal ring electronic system. We consider both the cases of linear Hall effect, the Berry curvature induced anomalous Hall and the perpendicular magnetic field-induced integer quantum Hall effect. We observe that the system exhibits a topological gap along the boundary of the nodal ring that strongly depends on the radius of the nodal ring and the strength of the Rashba spin-orbit interaction, resulting in the quantum anomalous Hall effect. Subsequently, we include a perpendicular uniform magnetic field and obtain the exact Landau levels that exhibit sign reversal in the slope with magnetic field around the ring boundary. This causes a sign reversal in the quantum Hall conductivity in the same band by tuning the magnetic field. Most importantly, we also show that when the magnetic field is strictly parallel to the system, it can induce an anisotropy to the Berry curvature that leads to the emergence of the nonlinear Hall effect. Additionally, tuning the parallel field can close and reopen the topological gap, resulting in the reverse topological phase transition from the topological Chern insulator to a trivial insulator. The nonlinear Hall effect remains persistent in both phases, but its dependence on the chemical potential exhibits distinct signatures. Noticeably, the nonlinear Hall response exhibits a sign-changing peak structure in Chern insulating phase whereas it displays a single-sign response in trivial insulating phase. These distinct signatures suggest that nonlinear Hall can serve as a probe to study such a topological phase transition.

</details>


### [106] [1D Interface-Induced Real-Space Berry Curvature Gradient Encoded in SpinValley-Coupled Nonlinear Photocurrent in Lateral Heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14993)
*Suman Kumar Chakraborty,Jinu James,Biswajeet Nayak,David Tebbe,Purbasha Ray,Christoph Stampfer,Bernd Beschoten,Lutz Waldecker,Prasana Kumar Sahoo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在CVD生长的单层二维横向异质结中，通过跨一维异质界面的非局域光电流测量，观察到由界面诱导贝里曲率不对称（贝里曲率偶极）驱动的自旋-谷耦合CPGE主导的螺旋度依赖光电流，并在MoSe2、WSe2及界面区域呈现不同幅值和极性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维TMD中圆偏振光电流通常需斜入射并由圆光子曳引效应产生，而正入射下的本征圆光电流效应（CPGE）仍难实现；因此需要探索对称性破缺的新平台以产生和调控螺旋度依赖的光电响应。

Method: 制备CVD单层二维横向异质结（LHS），测量跨一维异质界面的非局域光电流；结合空间与光谱分辨测量，选择性激发MoSe2、WSe2和界面区域，分析CPG响应的幅值与极性。

Result: 观察到清晰的CPGE信号，其螺旋度依赖光电流与界面诱导贝里曲率不对称驱动的自旋-谷耦合CPGE机制一致；MoSe2和WSe2表现出相反的自旋-谷二色性，一维界面处出现增强的谷混合，不同区域的CPG响应幅值和极性不同。

Conclusion: 该工作确立LHS为实现异质界面诱导CPGE类响应的稳健平台，并展示其在谷电子学和光自旋电子学中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Symmetry breaking enables helicity-dependent optoelectronic responses in quantum materials. In two-dimensional transition-metal dichalcogenides (TMDs), circular photocurrent (CPC) is typically observed under oblique illumination via the circular photon drag effect, while realization of the intrinsic circular photogalvanic effect (CPGE) under normal incidence remains challenging. Here, we report a clear signature of CPGE in chemical vapor deposition-grown monolayer 2D lateral heterostructure (LHS). By probing the nonlocal photocurrent across the 1D hetero-interface, we observe a helicity-dependent photocurrent consistent with a spin-valley-coupled CPGE-dominated mechanism driven by interface-induced Berry-curvature asymmetry, i.e., Berry curvature dipole. Spatially and spectrally resolved measurements under selective excitation of the MoSe2, WSe2, and the interface regions exhibit distinct magnitudes and polarities of the CPG responses. These contrasting behaviors highlight the opposite spin-valley dichroism for MoSe2 and WSe2, as well as enhanced valley mixing at the 1D interface. Our findings establish LHS as a robust platform for realizing a hetero-interface-induced CPGE-like response and highlight its potential for valleytronics and optospintronics.

</details>


### [107] [Quasilocal transport in the integer quantised Hall effect: adiabatic separation, local Ohm's law, and Joule heating of the incompressible strips](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15231)
*A. Siddiki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文以半经典图像重审整数量子霍尔输运，核心是将快速量子化回旋运动与慢变、自洽屏蔽的导心势场分离，并据此讨论电流分布、交流响应、非线性边界效应及热稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视整数量子霍尔输运的半经典路径，理解不可压缩条带如何吸引电流、霍尔平台如何受电流反馈影响，以及低场侧窄电流条带可能的热效应。

Method: 对正则作用量作 Bohr--Sommerfeld 量子化得到 Landau 谱；用 Born--Oppenheimer 绝热近似处理平滑限制势，绝热参数为 ℓ|∇V|/ℏω_c；由屏蔽给出自洽场。输运采用准局域形式 R_H=∫ρ_H j_y dx/∫j_y dx，j_y∝1/ρ_l，并在正则化局部 Ohm 模型中计算电流与 Joule 功率。进一步将电流反馈到静电问题，并在冻结剖面后处理中采用 Akera 启发的局域横向热闭合及两种声子损失律。

Result: 线性响应中冻结剖面模型的固有静电 AC 修正为反应性：Im R_H∝ω，Re R_H−R_H^DC∝ω²，且在 2 MHz 以下小于 10^-9。1--5 μA 电流反馈下，驱动边界条件打破样品反射对称，霍尔电压降集中到单一 strip，电流反向时该不对称性可交换至 10^-9；平台从低场侧变窄，而所研究 GaAs 参数下高场边缘保持在 ν(0)=2。热后处理表明低场侧窄电流条带可能被加热到晶格温度量级；采用活化热容闭合时，冷 strip 在 0.3--2 μA 的模型依赖电流下失去稳态。

Conclusion: 该半经典自洽框架可统一描述整数量子霍尔输运中的电流吸引、交流修正、非线性对称性破缺与平台边缘行为，并提供热估计；但热结论受声子损失律和冻结剖面假设限制，应视为估算而非定量预言。

Abstract: We reassess the semiclassical route to integer quantum Hall transport around one idea: the separation of fast, quantised cyclotron motion from a slow, self-consistently screened guiding-centre landscape. Bohr--Sommerfeld quantisation of the canonical action fixes the Landau spectrum; smooth confinement turns it into Born--Oppenheimer surfaces with adiabatic parameter $\ell|\nabla V|/\hbar\omega_c$; screening supplies the self-consistent field. Transport is quasilocal, $R_H=\int\rho_Hj_y\,dx/\int j_y\,dx$ with $j_y\propto1/\rho_l$, so within the regularised local Ohm model the incompressible strips attract the current by weight and the computed Joule-power profile follows the current profile. In linear response the intrinsic electrostatic AC correction of the frozen-profile model is reactive, $\mathrm{Im}\,R_H\propto\omega$, with $\mathrm{Re}\,R_H-R_H^{\rm DC}\propto\omega^{2}$ below $10^{-9}$ up to $2$~MHz. With the current fed back into the electrostatics at $1$--$5\,\mu$A, where the Hall drop is one to five cyclotron gaps, the driven boundary condition breaks the reflection symmetry of the bar, the Hall drop concentrates in one strip (interchanged to $10^{-9}$ under current reversal), and the plateau narrows from its low-field flank while, for the GaAs parameters studied, its high-field edge stays at $\nu(0)=2$. A local transverse thermal closure inspired by Akera, evaluated as a post-process on the frozen profile with two phonon-loss laws, indicates that the narrow current-carrying strips of the low-field flank may heat by of order the lattice temperature; with the activated-heat-capacity closure the cold strip loses its stationary solution at model-dependent currents of $0.3$--$2\,\mu$A. The thermal conclusions are bounded by the loss law and by the frozen profile, and are presented as estimates.

</details>


### [108] [Unified contact-free formulation of linear-response transport theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15371)
*Ziqian Wang,Ji Feng*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种统一的免接触项矩量线性响应公式：以标量源和推迟矩-矩关联函数的低频留数直接给出输运系数，并用于磁子热霍尔与自旋能斯特效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热霍尔输运通常写成电流-电流Kubo响应，并需借助辅助赝磁场引入能量-磁化修正；本文旨在简化并统一粒子、热和自旋输运的组织方式，避免单独的磁化减除与接触项修正。

Method: 针对直流极限下源贡献消失的通道，将输运系数表示为推迟矩-矩关联函数的低频留数，并在取直流极限前纳入等时端点贡献；公式仅使用标量源，不依赖局域电流规范，且在键能或相互作用能的可允许重分布下不变；通过源-矩响应矩阵统一处理粒子、grand-energy和指定自旋密度通道；以二次Landau-Lifshitz自旋波为例，用极化矩关联的长期增长隔离有限霍尔留数。

Result: 磁子热霍尔系数与自旋能斯特系数可由同一paraunitary能带几何得到。

Conclusion: 该算子框架统一了粒子、热和自旋输运的描述，可消除单独接触项和磁化修正，从而简化相关计算。

Abstract: Thermal Hall transport is conventionally formulated as a current--current Kubo response supplemented by an energy-magnetization correction obtained through an auxiliary pseudogravitational magnetic field. Motivated by this structure, we develop a unified contact-free moment formulation of linear response to scalar sources. For channels whose source contributions vanish in the dc limit, the transport coefficient is the low-frequency residue of a retarded moment--moment correlator, with equal-time endpoint contributions incorporated before the dc limit is taken. The formulation uses only scalar sources, requires no separate magnetization subtraction, is independent of the local-current gauge, and is invariant under admissible redistributions of bond or interaction energy. It organizes particle, grand-energy, and specified spin-density channels within a common source--moment response matrix. Quadratic Landau--Lifshitz spin waves illustrate the method: the secular growth of polarization-moment correlations isolates the finite Hall residue and yields the magnon thermal Hall and spin Nernst coefficients from the same paraunitary band geometry. The formulation provides a unified operator framework that simplifies the organization of particle, thermal, and spin transport and can streamline calculations by eliminating separate contact and magnetization corrections.

</details>


### [109] [Optical activation of nonlinear Hall effect in topological insulators with warped Fermi surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15460)
*Mohammad Shafiei,Farhad Fazileh,Milorad V. Milošević*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 六角翘曲拓扑绝缘体中，反演对称性破缺不足以产生非线性霍尔效应，因为三重旋转对称性使平衡贝里曲率偶极（BCD）恒为零；线偏振光的非共振 Floquet 工程可降低旋转对称性并保持时间反演对称性，从而诱导出可调 BCD，并在 Bi2Te3 中产生微安级非线性霍尔电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑绝缘体表面的六角翘曲费米面通常被视为非线性霍尔效应的天然平台，但作者指出仅靠翘曲导致的反演对称性破缺仍不足，因为体系保留的三重旋转对称性会强制平衡态 BCD 消失。因此需要寻找在不破坏时间反演对称性的前提下激活该对称性禁戒非线性输运的机制。

Method: 基于对称性分析和 Floquet 理论，研究六角翘曲拓扑绝缘体表面的贝里曲率与 BCD。引入非共振线偏振光，通过周期性驱动降低体系旋转对称性但保持时间反演对称性；计算光强和偏振对 BCD 大小、方向与符号的调控，并用真实 Bi2Te3 参数估算非线性霍尔电流。

Result: 平衡态下，尽管翘曲产生强各向异性贝里曲率，三重旋转对称性使 BCD 严格为零。在线偏振光 Floquet 区域，体系获得有限 BCD，其大小、方向和符号可由光强与偏振连续调节。对 Bi2Te3 实际参数，诱导 BCD 可达约 0.03 nm，并在实验可及条件下产生微安量级非线性霍尔电流。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了 Floquet 对称工程作为激活拓扑绝缘体表面被对称性禁戒非线性输运的有效途径，且无需破坏时间反演对称性。

Abstract: Topological insulators (TIs) with hexagonally warped Fermi surface are natural platforms for the nonlinear Hall effect, as warping breaks inversion symmetry while preserving time-reversal symmetry (TRS). Here we show that this inversion breaking alone is insufficient: although warping generates a strongly anisotropic Berry curvature, the preserved threefold rotational symmetry forces the equilibrium Berry curvature dipole (BCD) to vanish identically. We demonstrate that linearly polarized light removes this symmetry obstruction: in the off-resonant Floquet regime, it lowers the rotational symmetry while preserving TRS, thereby generating a finite BCD whose magnitude, orientation, and sign are continuously tunable by the light intensity and polarization. For realistic Bi$_2$Te$_3$ parameters, we show that the induced BCD reaches $\sim$0.03~nm, yielding microampere-scale nonlinear Hall currents under experimentally accessible conditions. Our results therefore establish Floquet symmetry engineering as a route to activating the symmetry-forbidden nonlinear transport on TI surfaces without breaking the TRS.

</details>


### [110] [Two Strategies to Measure Spin-Orbit-Torque Efficiency Acting on the Insulating Magnet Li$_{0.5}$Al$_{0.7}$Fe$_{1.8}$O$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15474)
*Orion Smedley,Thow Min Jerald Cham,Daisy O'Mahoney,Sanyum Channa,Xin Yu Zheng,Anna Janni,Lauren J. Riddiford,Yuhan Liang,Bozo Vareskic,Zbigniew Galazka,Yunqiu Kelly Luo,Yuri Suzuki,Daniel C. Ralph*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文比较了测量绝缘磁体中自旋轨道矩效率的两种策略，并以Pt/LAFO薄膜为例，指出ST-FMR可给出较可靠结果，而Sagnac干涉测量可能存在额外信号贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝缘磁体上用传统电学方法测自旋轨道矩效率时信噪比差且易受伪影影响，常规MOKE成像又常灵敏度不足。

Method: 采用横向+纵向自旋矩铁磁共振(ST-FMR)，以分离自旋矩信号与自旋泵浦、共振加热等伪影；并尝试用Sagnac干涉法光学测量自旋轨道矩引起的磁偏转，样品为Pt作用于14.4 nm LAFO薄膜。

Result: ST-FMR测得阻尼类自旋矩效率ξ_ST-FMR_DL ≈ 0.07，与Pt作用于金属铁磁体相当但略低；Sagnac干涉对14.4 nm LAFO薄膜具有足够灵敏度，但其推断的扭矩效率远弱于ST-FMR结果。

Conclusion: 作者推测Sagnac信号可能除了电流诱导磁偏转外还有其它贡献，例如巡游电子自旋的面外分量；这在磁光耦合系数很弱的绝缘磁体中尤为重要。

Abstract: For insulating magnets, widely-used electrically-based techniques to measure spin-orbit-torque efficiency can suffer from poor signal-to-noise ratios and unwanted artifacts, while optical measurements based on conventional magneto-optical Kerr effect (MOKE) imaging often lack sufficient sensitivity. Here we analyze two strategies for measuring the spin-torque efficiency acting on an insulating magnet, focusing on the torque from Pt acting on thin films of the low-damping magnet lithium aluminum ferrite (LAFO). First, lateral + longitudinal spin-torque ferromagnetic resonance (ST-FMR) allows separation of the spin-torque signal from artifacts due to spin-pumping and resonant heating. With this technique, we measure a spin-torque efficiency $\xi^\text{ST-FMR}_\text{DL}\sim\ 0.07$, comparable to but a bit less than for Pt acting on metallic ferromagnets. Second, we attempted optical measurements of spin-orbit-torque-induced magnetic deflection using Sagnac interferometry. We find that Sagnac interferometry provides sufficient sensitivity to measure signals from 14.4 nm LAFO thin films, but the inferred torque efficiency is much weaker than the result from lateral + longitudinal ST-FMR. We speculate that there may be another contribution to the Sagnac signal other than current-induced magnetic deflection, perhaps an out-of-plane component of itinerant electron spins. This becomes relevant for insulating magnets in which the magneto-optic coupling coefficient is very weak.

</details>


### [111] [Percolation threshold in cluster-assembled nanogranular palladium films and related strategies for hydrogen detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15629)
*A. Debot,D. Balakrishnan,A.M. Philippe,E. Barborini*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了超音速团簇束沉积制备的钯纳米颗粒膜在生长及氢暴露过程中的电输运，并探索了将钯团簇植入软聚合物以构建微型化氢检测和多模态传感的新换能机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解氢诱导的Pd团簇结构变化如何调制电输运，并利用该效应实现选择性氢检测；同时通过将Pd团簇植入软聚合物来促进团簇分离、增强量子隧穿，探索替代性气体传感机制。

Method: 采用超音速团簇束沉积原位监测团簇组装纳米颗粒Pd膜生长中的电输运，确定渗流行为；随后将2 nm（渗流厚度）和5 nm（渗流结束后）薄膜在0.25% H2/空气中非原位暴露；并将Pd团簇植入苯乙烯-乙烯-丁烯-苯乙烯（SEBS）软聚合物中研究其电输运。

Result: 渗流起始于1.5 nm，完成于2.5 nm；薄膜在空气中基线稳定，氢响应快（T50% < 10 s）；氢暴露引起的电导变化包含可逆和不可逆组分，不可逆组分暂归因于氢促进的Pd团簇部分聚并；聚合物植入样品中，氢诱导的聚合物溶胀主导传感行为，而非Pd结构修饰主导。

Conclusion: 结果表明，Pd团簇结构变化和聚合物溶胀均可作为氢检测换能机制，为微型化氢检测和多模态传感提供了新的可能路径。

Abstract: Electrical transport in cluster-assembled nanogranular palladium films was investigated in-situ during growth by Supersonic Cluster Beam Deposition. The percolation onset is observed at a thickness of 1.5 nm, while the percolation completion at 2.5 nm. Films at the percolation thickness (2 nm) and beyond the percolation end (5 nm) were subsequently exposed ex-situ to 0.25% hydrogen in air to investigate how hydrogen-induced structural changes in Pd clusters modulate electrical transport and can be exploited as mechanism for selective hydrogen detection. Implantation of palladium clusters in soft polymer styrene-ethylene-butylene-styrene was also explored to promote clusters separation and enhance quantum tunnelling phenomena within electrical transport. The observed electrical transport pattern features stable baselines in air, suggesting material stability, fast response time (T50% < 10 s), and the coexistence of reversible and irreversible components in conduction changes induced by hydrogen exposure. The irreversible component is tentatively attributed to hydrogen-promoted partial coalescence of Pd clusters. The behaviour of the polymer implanted samples suggests an alternative gas detection mechanism where hydrogen-induced polymer swelling dominates over palladium structural modifications. These results disclose novel possible transducing routes for miniaturized hydrogen detection and multimodal sensing.

</details>


### [112] [Opto-Electrical Detection of Donor Bound Excitons in Silicon-on-Insulator Substrate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15674)
*A. Kanniainen,A. S. Kumar,A. Sammak,G. Scappucci,J. T. Muhonen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在SOI材料（包括同位素纯化28-Si器件层）中首次实现施主束缚激子的系综光电测量，并观察到相对体硅的共振波长显著偏移与线宽展宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅中施主束缚电子自旋是相干性良好的量子比特，但缺乏相干光学接口；施主束缚激子可被光学激发并通过俄歇复合产生电信号，从而提供光电自旋读出路径。将该读出扩展到单自旋水平需与硅光子学集成，进而需要采用SOI衬底。

Method: 在SOI材料上，包括同位素纯化28-Si器件层，对施主束缚激子进行系综级光电测量，并与体硅实验结果进行比较。

Result: 实验观测到施主束缚激子跃迁的共振波长发生显著偏移，且跃迁线宽出现展宽，与体材料实验结果明显不同。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在SOI中开展施主束缚激子光电测量的可行性，为SOI硅光子学集成的自旋读出提供了基础；但SOI引入的共振波长偏移和线宽展宽需要进一步理解与控制。

Abstract: Spin of a donor bound electron in silicon has been shown to be a very coherent qubit system but lacks a coherent optical interface. There does, however, exist a donor bound exciton transition that can be excited optically but decays dominantly via an Auger recombination producing an electrical signal. This provides an opto-electrical pathway for spin readout. Scaling this readout to single-spin level will require interfacing the spins with silicon photonics for efficient guiding of photons, which in turn will require moving to silicon-on-insulator (SOI) substrates. Here we demonstrate first ensemble opto-electronic measurements of donor bound excitons in SOI material, including isotopically purified 28-Si device layers. The experiments show pronounced shifts in the resonance wavelengths and broadenings of the transition linewidths compared to the bulk experiments.

</details>


### [113] [A self-compliant selector-free memory with functional bismuth ferrite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15732)
*Suman Roy,Subhabrata Das,Priyanka Sahu,Majid Ahmadi,Sameer Kumar Mallik,Kaushik Ghosh,Saroj Prasad Dash,Satyaprakash Sahoo*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文基于多铁材料BiFeO3构建了无选择器、免形成、稳定的多阻态忆阻器交叉阵列，利用自然形成的TiOx界面层实现自限流，在良率、耐久性、保持性和4位多值存储方面表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内存计算迫切需要无选择器操作、低变异性和稳定多值开关，但现有阻变存储体系难以在同一系统中同时满足这些要求，限制了其实际部署。

Method: 制备BiFeO3忆阻器交叉阵列；通过自然形成的TiOx界面层获得自限流特性，实现免形成双极性阻变；采用脉冲控制编程实现多电导态；并进行噪声测量与阵列可靠性评估。

Result: 阵列实现稳定双极性开关，器件良率达97%；耐久性超过10^5次循环，数据保持超过10^5秒，且循环间与器件间变异性低；具备16个分离良好的电导态，可实现4位多值操作并用于边缘应用中的数字可视化；噪声以1/f型为主，随机电报噪声极小。

Conclusion: BiFeO3可作为实现多比特、无选择器交叉阵列存储器的实用材料平台，兼具高可靠性和可扩展性，适用于基于功能材料的可扩展计算架构。

Abstract: In-memory computing requires selector-free operation, low variability, and stable multilevel switching, all of which remain challenging to achieve in a single system. Here, we demonstrate a robust selector-free memory based on a multiferroic BiFeO3 that combines self-compliance with stable bipolar switching, high yield, long endurance, and extended retention. We observe that BiFeO3 memristive crossbar arrays become self-compliant through a naturally formed TiOx interfacial layer, enabling forming-free bipolar switching without external selector devices, addressing major bottlenecks that have limited the practical deployment of resistive memories. The array delivers stable bipolar switching with a 97% device yield, endurance beyond 105 cycles, and data retention exceeding 105 s with low cycle-to-cycle and device-to-device variability. In addition, the devices exhibit 16 well-separated conductance states, enabling 4-bit multilevel operation via precise pulse-controlled programming for digit visualization in edge applications. Noise measurements reveal predominantly 1/f-type behavior with minimal random telegraph noise, indicating robust conductance stability across the programmable window. These results establish BiFeO3 as a practical materials platform for multibit, selector-free crossbar memories offering high reliability, making it well-suited for scalable computing architectures based on functional materials.

</details>


### [114] [Effects of residual exchange coupling on simultaneously driven spin qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15751)
*Heun Mo Yoo,Tanner M. Janda,Victor Yu,Michael J. Gullans,Adam R. Mills,Jason R. Petta*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究了交换耦合与微波驱动共同作用下双自旋量子比特的动力学，发现Rabi频率与交换耦合的相对大小决定拍频或交换劈裂图案，且Rabi频率差超过交换耦合时效应被抑制，理论模型复现了主要特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交换耦合和微波驱动是自旋量子比特的常用控制机制，但交换耦合如何影响微波驱动的自旋动力学尚不完全清楚。

Method: 对两个自旋量子比特同时施加微波驱动，测量其随交换耦合强度和驱动功率变化的Rabi振荡与Rabi chevrons，并建立理论模型进行对比。

Result: 当Rabi频率超过交换耦合时，Rabi振荡出现拍频；在相反极限下，Rabi chevrons中出现两个交换劈裂图案；当两个Rabi频率之差超过交换耦合时，这些交换诱导效应被抑制。

Conclusion: 实验揭示了交换耦合与微波驱动相互作用的规律，理论模型能复现主要实验特征，有助于理解并优化自旋量子比特的相干控制。

Abstract: Exchange coupling and microwave drives are widely used control mechanisms for spin qubits. However, the influence of exchange coupling on microwave-driven spin dynamics is not fully understood. We report simultaneous drive measurements of two spin qubits as a function of exchange coupling and drive power. When the Rabi frequency exceeds exchange, we observe a beating pattern in the Rabi oscillations. In the opposite limit, two exchange-split patterns emerge in the Rabi chevrons. We find that these exchange-induced effects are suppressed when the difference in the Rabi frequencies exceeds the exchange coupling. A theoretical model is developed that reproduces the main features in our data.

</details>


### [115] [Spatiotemporal programming via asymmetric dielectric engineering for nonvolatile 2D optoelectronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15771)
*Xiaoguang Luo,Xiaolong Zhang,Honglei Chen,Jiaming Wang,Fan Liu,Jiongtao Zhang,Junqiang Zhang,Yihan Yin,Jinpeng Xu,Lei Ying,Renjing Xu,Yingchun Cheng,Xuetao Gan,Wei Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出一种双浮栅架构与对称性破缺隧穿介质的时空编程策略，在单个输入栅极下实现二维半导体沟道中非易失、可逆的p-n与n-p同质结切换，并用于多功能器件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双极性二维半导体与浮栅架构有望实现非易失、可重构电子器件，但传统p-n与n-p极性切换依赖复杂多栅设计，限制可扩展性与集成密度。

Method: 构建双浮栅架构，并采用对称性破缺的隧穿介质；非对称介质堆叠为两个浮栅引入不同隧穿阈值，使单一输入栅极可通过定义的电压脉冲序列编码沟道中的空间掺杂分布。

Result: 实现按需、非易失、可逆的p-n与n-p配置切换，具有优异保持性与耐久性；可重构同质结可用于逻辑编码、整流、光电探测及传感计算。

Conclusion: 该工作以时空编程替代空间输入复杂性，建立高密度、多功能智能硬件的新设计范式。

Abstract: Ambipolar two dimensional (2D) semiconductors integrated with floating-gate architectures offer a promising platform for nonvolatile, reconfigurable electronics. However, the switching between p-n and n-p junction polarities has conventionally required complex multi-gate designs, hindering the scalability and integration density. Here, we demonstrate a spatiotemporal programming strategy using a dual-floating-gate architecture with a symmetry broken tunneling dielectric. An asymmetric dielectric stack creates distinct tunneling thresholds for two floating gates, enabling a single input gate to encode spatial doping profiles in the 2D channel via defined voltage pulse sequences. We achieve on demand, nonvolatile, and reversible switching between p-n and n-p configurations with excellent retention and endurance. The reconfigurable homojunction serves as a multifunctional platform for logic encoding, rectification, photodetection, and in sensor computing. This work establishes a design paradigm that replaces spatial input complexity with spatiotemporal programming, paving the way for high-density, multifunctional intelligent hardware.

</details>


### [116] [Induced superconductivity in selective-area grown SnTe devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15778)
*Maarten J.G. Kamphuis,Yoran F.S. Starmans,Pim J.H. Lueb,Femke J. Witmans,Marvin M. Jansen-Zilles,Marcel A. Verheijen,Reinoud Lavrijsen,Joost Ridderbos,Fabrizio Nichele,Floris A. Zwanenburg,Erik P.A.M. Bakkers,Alexander Brinkman*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在InP衬底上选区生长SnTe拓扑晶体绝缘体纳米线，界面因In扩散形成超导InxSn1-xTe层；低温输运观察到Little-Parks振荡，但无拓扑超导特有的半周期移动，表明平庸输运通道掩盖了拓扑信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑量子计算需要拓扑超导态，可通过在拓扑绝缘体/拓扑晶体绝缘体中近邻诱导超导来实现，因此需要研究SnTe/InP体系中的诱导超导及其拓扑性质。

Method: 采用选区生长制备InP衬底上的SnTe纳米线，结合TEM/EDX表征与低温电子输运测量，并测量环形纳米线网络在外加垂直磁场下的Little-Parks振荡。

Result: 超导行为很可能源于衬底中的In向上扩散进入SnTe纳米线，在界面形成InxSn1-xTe；当In浓度高于2%时该材料本征超导。环形网络中观察到Little-Parks振荡，但未出现指示拓扑超导态的半个周期移动。

Conclusion: 该异质结构中的超导主要来自界面InxSn1-xTe，且所观测输运由平庸通道主导，掩盖了可能的拓扑超导特征；未获得拓扑超导的确凿证据。

Abstract: Topological superconductors are of high interest for applications in topological quantum computation. The required topologically superconducting state can be engineered by proximityinducing superconductivity in a topological insulator. Here, we explore the induced superconductivity in selective-area grown nanowires of the topological crystalline insulator SnTe on a InP substrate, through TEM/EDX and low-temperature electronic transport studies. The observed superconducting behavior likely originates from indium in the substrate diffusing upwards into the SnTe nanowire, forming a thin layer of InxSn1-xTe at the interface between the nanowire and the substrate. InxSn1-xTe is intrinsically superconducting for indium concentrations above 2%, resulting in superconductivity within the heterostructure. Little-Parks oscillations are observed in loop-shaped nanowire networks in an out-of-plane magnetic field. The half-period shift indicative of a topological superconducting state is absent, which is explained by dominant trivial transport channels obscuring any topological signatures.

</details>


### [117] [Up/down-conversion of infrared light by few-layer graphene polytypes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15866)
*Patrick Johansen Sarsfield,Takaaki V. Joya,Takuto Kawakami,Mikito Koshino,Vladimir Falko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 少层石墨烯，特别是非中心对称的ABCB四层和经非对称封装的菱形三/四层，在红外波段具有高效近共振的和频/下转换二阶非线性，并有望用于光纤内产生偏振关联光子对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 破缺反演对称性的材料可实现双光子上转换/下转换过程；研究旨在寻找红外波段具有高效非线性、且可集成到光纤中的二维材料平台。

Method: 识别并研究少层石墨烯薄膜，重点分析混合堆叠ABCB四层中的近共振上/下转换；同时考察通过非对称封装在菱形三/四层中促进二阶非线性。

Result: 在0.7 eV<Ω=ω1+ω2<1.1 eV范围内实现[ω1,ω2]光子对与Ω光子之间的高效近共振上/下转换；非对称封装的菱形三/四层在0.7–0.9 eV表现出显著二阶非线性。

Conclusion: 少层石墨烯涂层光纤可实现“光纤内”产生具有关联偏振的光子对。

Abstract: Optical nonlinearity of materials with broken inversion symmetry enables two-photon processes where an incoming pair of photons can generate an up-converted photon with the combined frequency, or a high-energy photon can be split into a correlated pair of down-converted photons. Here, we identify few-layer graphene films that offer up/down-conversion capability in the infrared spectral range. For mixed stacking tetralayers (ABCB), which are non-centrosymmetric 2D crystals, we find highly efficient nearly resonant up/down-conversion of [$\omega_1,\omega_2$] photon pairs into/from a photon with $0.7$eV$<\Omega=\omega_1+\omega_2<1.1$eV. We also note that a pronounced second-order nonlinearity in the spectral range of $0.7$eV$<\Omega<0.9$eV can be promoted in rhombohedral tri- and tetralayers by asymmetrical encapsulation. Potentially, for fibers coated with few-layer graphene, this opens the door for ``in fiber" production of photon pairs with correlated polarizations.

</details>


### [118] [Shot noise signatures of Majorana-assisted transport in an Aharonov-Bohm interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15966)
*Ignacio Román-Pérez,Juan Pablo Ramos-Andrade,Pedro Orellana,Luis Rosales*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究双量子点阿哈罗诺夫-玻姆干涉仪中，量子点侧耦合到含马约拉纳束缚态的拓扑超导纳米线时的有限偏压电流、散粒噪声与Fano因子，并提出散粒噪声谱可作为马约拉纳束缚态的灵敏间接探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马约拉纳束缚态在拓扑超导纳米线中的探测具有重要意义，但直接测量困难，因此需要寻找灵敏且间接的输运信号。

Method: 采用非平衡格林函数形式结合运动方程方法，计算两个平行量子点分别侧耦合到有效Kitaev链所描述的拓扑超导纳米线时的有限偏压电荷电流、散粒噪声和Fano因子。

Result: 耦合到马约拉纳束缚态会显著改变干涉图样，并在电流、散粒噪声和Fano因子中产生特征信号；这些信号在半通量条件下尤为明显，此时破坏性干涉抑制常规输运背景并增强马约拉纳辅助输运的可见度。

Conclusion: 有限偏压散粒噪声谱能够作为混合超导纳米线干涉仪中马约拉纳束缚态的敏感间接探测手段。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the finite-bias charge current, shot noise, and Fano factor in an Aharonov-Bohm interferometer composed of two parallel quantum dots, each side-coupled to a topological superconducting nanowire represented by an effective Kitaev chain hosting Majorana bound states at its ends. The transport properties are calculated using the nonequilibrium Green's function formalism within the equation-of-motion approach. Our results show that coupling to the Majorana bound states significantly modifies the interference pattern, producing distinctive signatures in the current, shot noise, and Fano factor. These signatures are particularly pronounced at the half-flux condition, where destructive interference suppresses the conventional transport background and enhances the visibility of Majorana-assisted transport. These findings demonstrate that finite-bias shot-noise spectroscopy provides a sensitive indirect probe of Majorana bound states in hybrid superconducting-nanowire interferometers.

</details>
