# cond-mat.str-el [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Effective theory of the hidden-order pseudogap phase in a doped antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13391)
*Gaia De Paciani,Gesa Dünnweber,Johannes Poersch,Simon M. Linsel,Henning Schlömer,Fabian Grusdt*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出用涨落条纹（弦状畴壁）描述掺杂反铁磁体的有效模型，并通过经典蒙特卡洛相图把畴壁端点的 BKT 型解禁闭交叉解释为铜氧化物赝能隙 T* 交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赝能隙相的微观起源及其与铜氧化物高温超导的关系长期未解，核心问题在于掺杂如何破坏反铁磁序并产生隐藏序、条纹涨落等竞争现象。

Method: 将 Néel 序中的条纹/畴壁开放端视为格点规范理论中的涡旋，构建掺杂反铁磁体的有效模型；对模型进行经典 Monte Carlo 数值模拟，并使用基于渗流的几何序参量诊断隐藏 Néel 序，从而确定相图。

Result: 高温下发现 BKT 型交叉，畴壁端点解禁闭，被解释为从隐藏序区到上方顺磁金属的 T* 交叉；低温下识别出条纹不稳定性。

Conclusion: 该有效模型为赝能隙、隐藏反铁磁序与条纹不稳定性提供了统一图像，其预测可在超冷费米子量子模拟器中检验。

Abstract: The microscopic origin of the pseudogap phase constitutes a longstanding puzzle related to the emergence of high-temperature superconductivity in cuprate materials. In this work, we develop an effective model for doped antiferromagnets in terms of fluctuating stripes, or string-like domain walls, which obscure the antiferromagnetic order of the spin background. The open ends of such domain walls of the N'eel order are treated as vortices in the resulting lattice gauge theory. We numerically evaluate the phase diagram by classical Monte Carlo simulations, using percolation-based geometric order parameters to diagnose hidden N'eel order. At high temperatures, we identify a BKT-type crossover in which the domain wall ends become deconfined. We interpret this as the $T^*$ crossover from the hidden order regime to the paramagnetic metal above. At low temperatures, we identify stripe instabilities. Predictions of our effective model can be tested in ultracold fermion quantum simulators.

</details>


### [120] [Tripartite entanglement in chaotic eigenstates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13411)
*Junjia Zhang,Ramanjit Sohal,Shinsei Ryu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究量子混沌本征态的三分体纠缠普适特征，用多熵和反射熵推导集合平均并验证三分体 ETH 推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典统计力学被认为从量子混沌系统的纠缠结构中涌现，但这一结论主要基于本征态的二分纠缠与子系统 ETH；多体态常具有多分纠缠，因此混沌本征态的普适多分纠缠特征仍不清楚。

Method: 研究遍历二分拟设的三分体推广，考察 Rényi 三分体多熵与 Rényi 反射熵，利用鞍点分析推导集合平均的领头阶解析表达式；同时计算控制 Rényi-2 反射熵的反射密度矩阵 ρ_{AA^*}^{(2)} 的平均，并与混合场 Ising 模型的精确对角化结果进行基准检验。

Result: 得到两个三分体纠缠探测量集合平均的领头阶解析式；证明混沌本征态中的 ρ_{AA^*}^{(2)} 可由该集合平均很好描述，且随子系统尺寸区间呈现三种定性不同形式，可视为子系统 ETH 的三分体推广；解析预测与混合场 Ising 模型精确对角化吻合。

Conclusion: 混沌本征态除二分纠缠外也具有可由集合平均刻画的普适三分体纠缠结构，支持三分体版本的 ETH/遍历二分拟设图景。

Abstract: It is now well-established that classical statistical mechanics emerges from the entanglement structure of quantum chaotic systems, as quantified by the (subsystem) eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH). While this statement rests on the well-studied bipartite entanglement of the eigenstates of such systems, recent years have shown that many-body states are often characterized by multipartite entanglement, raising the question: what are the universal multipartite entanglement features of chaotic eigenstates? We answer this question in part by studying a tripartite generalization of the ergodic bipartition ansatz through the lens of two tripartite entanglement probes, the R\'enyi tripartite multi-entropy and the R\'enyi reflected entropy. We derive leading-order analytical expressions for the ensemble averages of both quantities using a saddle-point analysis. Beyond these measures, we obtain the average of the R\'enyi-2 reflected density matrix, $\rho_{AA^*}^{(2)}$,which controls the R\'enyi-2 reflected entropy. We show that $\rho_{AA^*}^{(2)}$ in a chaotic eigenstate is well-described by this ensemble average which, unlike an ordinary reduced density matrix, takes three qualitatively distinct forms across subsystem-size regimes, which may be viewed as a tripartite generalization of subsystem ETH. We benchmark our analytical predictions against exact diagonalization of the mixed-field Ising model.

</details>


### [121] [Probing the anomalous symmetry-breaking in kagome material CsV3Sb5 via third-order nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13656)
*Zheng Dai,Fengyi Guo,Shuai Zhang,Xiubing Li,Congcong Li,Yufan Ju,Ziqi Wang,Bin Cheng,Zhe Ying,Fengqi Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在Kagome材料CsV3Sb5中观测到可稳定至室温的巨大三阶非线性纵向与霍尔响应，并在电荷密度波和反常对称破缺态下显著增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kagome AV3Sb5家族具有与拓扑电子能带相关的反常对称破缺电荷有序态，亟需有效手段探测该类对称性破缺并理解其非线性输运性质。

Method: 测量CsV3Sb5的三阶非线性纵向和霍尔响应，并对其进行标度分析，以区分量子几何贡献与外来散射机制。

Result: 三阶非线性响应稳定至室温，在电荷密度波（约77 K）和反常对称破缺（约39 K）态以下显著增强；标度分析表明三阶非线性输运由量子几何贡献和外来散射共同主导。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了巨大的三阶非线性响应，并提供了一种探测CsV3Sb5中反常对称破缺的独特方法。

Abstract: The kagome material has rapidly established itself as a research frontier in condensed matter physics, owing to its distinctive geometric structure and the rich array of unconventional physical phenomena. In the kagome AV3Sb5 (A = K, Rb, Cs) family, the charge-ordered state exhibits a remarkable characteristic, i.e., anomalous symmetry-breaking, which is tied to the topological nature of the electronic band structure. Here, we report the third-order nonlinear longitudinal and Hall responses that persist stably up to room temperature in the kagome material CsV3Sb5. Notably, the nonlinear responses demonstrate significant enhancement below the charge density wave (~ 77 K) order and anomalous symmetry-breaking (~ 39 K) state. The scaling analysis indicates that the third-order nonlinear transport is governed jointly by quantum geometric contribution and extrinsic scattering. This study realizes a giant third-order nonlinear response and provides a distinct method to detect anomalous symmetry-breaking in CsV3Sb5.

</details>


### [122] [Layer- and Orbital-Selective Mott Physics Driving Dimensional Crossover in Nickelate Superlattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13723)
*Minjae Kim,Byungmin Sohn,Sangjae Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一种由双层/单层LaNiO3与单层LaAlO3隔离层构成的镍酸盐异质结平台，可在压缩应变下实现层选择与轨道选择的Mott转变（LOSMT），并驱动维度交叉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子材料设计中，操控维度性与电子关联以探索涌现现象是核心目标；本文旨在构建可实现层-和轨道-选择性Mott转变的新型关联量子材料平台。

Method: 将双层LaNiO3和单层LaNiO3用单层LaAlO3隔离层分隔，并置于LaAlO3衬底提供的压缩应变下；采用密度泛函理论加动力学平均场理论（DFT+DMFT）进行计算。

Result: LOSMT源于 apical Ni d-z^2 轨道的层依赖维度限域和应变诱导的轨道简并解除。单层LaNiO3表现为轨道选择Mott绝缘相，Ni d-z^2轨道打开Mott能隙；双层LaNiO3表现为多轨道金属相。降温时，通过层间Ni d-z^2轨道杂化触发轨道选择Mott转变。该转变通过使局域化apical Ni d-z^2轨道金属化，实现面外电子输运并驱动维度交叉，而平面Ni d-x^2-y^2轨道在整个转变中保持 itinerant。

Conclusion: 该LOSMT为工程化关联量子材料提供了新路径，有望通向非常规超导和Mott晶体管功能。

Abstract: The design of quantum materials to manipulate dimensionality and electronic correlations is a central focus in the exploration of emergent phenomena. We propose a new correlated quantum material platform for realizing a layer- and orbital-selective Mott transition (LOSMT) in a nickelate heterostructure by combining bilayer and monolayer LaNiO$_3$ separated by a monolayer LaAlO$_3$ spacer under compressive strain from the LaAlO$_3$ substrate. The LOSMT emerges from the layer-dependent dimensional confinement of the apical Ni($d$)-$z^2$ orbital and strain-induced lifting of the orbital degeneracy. The monolayer LaNiO$_3$ exhibits an orbital-selective Mott insulating phase with a Mott gap in the Ni($d$)-$z^2$ orbital, whereas the bilayer LaNiO$_3$ exhibits a multi-orbital metallic phase, triggering the orbital-selective Mott transition upon cooling $via$ inter-layer hybridization of the Ni($d$)-$z^2$ orbitals. Calculations within the density functional theory plus dynamical mean-field theory (DFT+DMFT) framework reveal that the LOSMT drives a dimensional crossover by enabling out-of-plane electronic transport through the metallization of the localized apical Ni($d$)-$z^2$ orbital, while the planar Ni($d$)-$x^2-y^2$ orbital remains itinerant across the transition. The emergence of the LOSMT provides a new pathway for engineering correlated quantum materials toward unconventional superconductivity and Mott-transistor functionalities.

</details>


### [123] [Bilinear magnon--exciton coupling in biased ferromagnetic electron--hole bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13752)
*Pieter M. Gunnink*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出偏压铁磁电子-空穴双层平台，通过电压调节激子能隙，实现磁子与激子的共振耦合、线性杂化及磁子-激子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维磁性半导体可实现强磁子-激子相互作用，但激子与磁子固有能量失配使耦合通常仅涉及激子密度与磁子，难以共振耦合。

Method: 从电子与局域磁矩间的微观交换相互作用出发，研究偏压铁磁电子-空穴双层中激子能隙随电压降低时的磁子-激子耦合，并微观推导吸引性磁子-激子密度-密度相互作用。

Result: 在共振区证明磁子与自旋翻转激子发生线性杂化；进一步降低激子能量时形成磁子-激子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚，该凝聚受吸引性密度-密度相互作用修正，其集体模式与磁子杂化。

Conclusion: 该平台可实现可调的共振磁子-激子耦合与凝聚，集体模式-磁子杂化提供了探测激子凝聚形成的替代机制。

Abstract: Two-dimensional magnetic semiconductors provide a unique platform where strong magnon--exciton interactions can be realized. Owing to the intrinsic energy mismatch between excitons and magnons, however, these interactions are typically between the exciton density and magnons. We propose biased ferromagnetic electron--hole bilayers, where the exciton energy gap can be lowered with the voltage bias, as a platform in which excitons and magnons can instead couple resonantly. Starting from the microscopic exchange interactions between electrons and localized magnetic moments, we demonstrate the linear hybridization of magnons and spin-flip excitons in this regime. As the exciton energy is lowered further, a magnon--exciton Bose--Einstein condensate is formed. We show that this condensate is further modified by an attractive magnon--exciton density--density interaction, which we derive microscopically. We study the condensate's collective modes and demonstrate their hybridization with the magnons, providing an alternative detection mechanism for the formation of exciton condensates.

</details>


### [124] [Exact solutions of hidden free fermion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14134)
*Mingchen Zheng,Balázs Pozsgay,Shu Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文揭示隐藏自由费米子（HFF）模型的代数起源：它们可由扭曲多态 Perk–Schultz R 矩阵的不同 Lax 算符实现导出，并通过反射退化生成的有限对易转移矩阵层级及函数关系精确求解；以非均匀 FFD 链为例，得到依赖耦合的谱分类及边界条件下涌现立方有限尺寸能隙，同时保留 z=3/2 体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HFF 哈密顿量虽呈自由费米子谱却无显式二次形式，其代数来源及周期边界行为尚未明确。

Method: 基于扭曲多态 Perk–Schultz R 矩阵的不同 Lax 算符实现；利用其反射退化构造有限互相对易的转移矩阵层级，并借助函数关系确定完整多体谱；求解非均匀 free-fermions-in-disguise（FFD）链。

Result: 证明一大类 HFF 模型源于该 R 矩阵的 Lax 实现；非均匀 FFD 链依耦合呈现 HFF、嵌套 HFF 或相互作用谱；均匀 FFD 链在一绕与周期边界下出现涌现立方有限尺寸能隙，并保持精确 z=3/2 体行为。

Conclusion: 该构造可推广到更高态和面型实现，为相互作用量子系统中依赖耦合的谱组织及多尺度行为提供统一代数框架。

Abstract: Hidden-free-fermion (HFF) Hamiltonians exhibit free-fermion spectra despite lacking an explicit quadratic representation, yet their algebraic provenance and periodic-boundary behavior remain open questions. Here we establish that a broad class of HFF models arises from distinct Lax operator realizations of a twisted multistate Perk--Schultz \(R\) matrix. Its reflected degeneration generates a closed finite hierarchy of mutually commuting transfer matrices, whose functional relations determine the complete many-body spectrum. As a decisive example, we solve the inhomogeneous free-fermions-in-disguise (FFD) chain, which exhibits HFF, nested HFF, or interacting spectra, depending on its coupling. The homogeneous FFD chain with both one-wrap and periodic boundary conditions exhibits an emergent cubic finite-size gap while retaining the exact \(z=3/2\) bulk behavior. The construction extends to higher-state and face-type realizations, providing a common algebraic framework for coupling-dependent spectral organization and multiscale behavior in interacting quantum systems.

</details>


### [125] [Haldane-Holstein model at fractional filling: Route to bosonic fractional Chern insulator and quantum anomalous Hall crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14191)
*Zezhu Wei,Ang-Kun Wu,Di Xiao,Shi-Zeng Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 电子-声子耦合通常被认为会抑制能带拓扑，但本文表明，在部分填充的拓扑陈带中，强电子-声子耦合反而可稳定多种拓扑物态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战“电声耦合破坏拓扑”的传统认识，探索其在部分填充陈带中诱导或稳定拓扑相的可能性。

Method: 以原型 Haldane-Holstein 模型为平台，考虑拓扑带的部分填充，并调节能带拓扑与电子-声子耦合强度，构建并分析相图。

Result: 耦合既产生关联载流子的长程相互作用，又在吸引通道中将其束缚为玻色对。自旋电子情形下，相反自旋配对成电荷-2e 双极化子，并在 ν=1/2 形成带电荷-e 半子激发的玻色分数量子陈绝缘体；相图还包含在 M、K 点凝聚的超导体及多种电荷有序固体。自旋极化电子情形下，强耦合在 ν=1/3 稳定 C=2 的量子反常霍尔晶体。

Conclusion: 电子-声子耦合可成为在部分填充陈带中实现拓扑的途径，而非拓扑的障碍。

Abstract: Electron-phonon coupling is generally expected to suppress band topology, driving a topological band insulator into a trivial phase. Here we show that, using the prototypical Haldane-Holstein model at partial filling of a topological band, strong electron-phonon coupling can instead stabilize topological phases of matter. The electron-phonon coupling plays a double role: it generates the longer-range interactions that correlate the carriers and, in the attractive channel, also binds them into bosonic pairs. For spinful electrons, it pairs opposite spins into charge-$2e$ bipolarons that form a bosonic fractional Chern insulator (FCI) at filling $\nu=1/2$ with charge-$e$ semionic excitations. Tuning the band topology and the coupling strength maps out a rich phase diagram containing this bosonic FCI, superconductors condensed at the $M$ and $K$ points, and several charge-ordered solids. For spin-polarized electrons, strong coupling instead stabilizes a $C=2$ quantum anomalous Hall crystal (QAHC) at $\nu=1/3$. Electron-phonon coupling thus emerges as a route to, rather than an obstruction against, topology in partially filled Chern bands.

</details>


### [126] [Emergence of Subdominant Quadrupolar Order in the 5$d^2$ Multipolar Mott Insulator Ba$_2$CaOsO$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14223)
*Ilija K. Nikolov,Giuseppe Allodi,Ginevra Corsale,Anshu Kataria,Cierra Foster,Patrick Woodward,Samuele Sanna,Vesna F. Mitrović*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在纯净5d² Ba₂CaOsO₆中，⁴³Ca NMR发现T*≈50 K出现破缺局域点对称性（BLPS）相，并与八极序转变重合；该隐藏序本质上是多分量（八极+次主导四极）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掺杂Ba₂Na₀.₁Ca₀.₉OsO₆中BLPS是否由化学无序诱导，还是洁净5d²极限的本征性质，此前尚不清楚。

Method: 对纯净Ba₂CaOsO₆进行⁴³Ca核磁共振谱分析，并用自旋回波章动测量探测电场梯度（EFG），以检验平均立方结构下的局域对称破缺。

Result: 在Ba₂CaOsO₆中于T*≈50 K发现BLPS相，与八极转变精确重合；章动测量探测到有限EFG，证明尽管平均结构为立方，仍发生自发对称破缺。数据支持八极序，同时揭示次主导四极贡献，表明隐藏序为内在多分量。

Conclusion: 局域对称破缺是洁净5d²极限的本征性质；混合多极态超出常规5d²基态理论，为强自旋轨道耦合Mott绝缘体的微观理论提供新约束。

Abstract: Relativistic $5d$ Mott insulators host a rich variety of exotic hidden multipolar order, whose microscopic nature remains heavily debated. A broken local point-symmetry (BLPS) phase followed by antiferromagnetism was previously observed in the doped compound Ba$_2$Na$_{0.1}$Ca$_{0.9}$OsO$_6$, where mixed Os valence places the system toward the $5d^2$ end of the $5d^1$-$5d^2$ regime. In contrast, pristine $5d^2$ Ba$_2$CaOsO$_6$ has shown a single octupolar transition with average cubic symmetry, raising the question of whether BLPS arises from chemical disorder in the doped systems. Here, we discover the BLPS phase in pristine Ba$_2$CaOsO$_6$ emerging at $T^* \approx 50$ K, precisely coincident with the octupolar transition. Beyond standard ${}^{43}$Ca nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectral analysis, our spin-echo nutation measurements detect a finite electric field gradient, demonstrating spontaneous symmetry breaking despite the cubic average structure. While our NMR data support the previously identified octupolar order, the BLPS phase reveals a subdominant quadrupolar contribution, establishing that the hidden order in Ba$_2$CaOsO$_6$ is intrinsically multicomponent. Such mixed multipolar states lie beyond conventional theoretical descriptions of the $5d^2$ ground state. Our results establish local symmetry breaking as an intrinsic property of the clean $5d^2$ limit and provide a new constraint on microscopic theories of strongly spin-orbit-coupled Mott insulators.

</details>


### [127] [Charge order before superconductivity in the doped kagome Dirac spin liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15947)
*Yasir Iqbal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在kagome晶格中掺杂U(1) Dirac自旋液体时，因线图能带效应而非相互作用，系统先形成电荷序；chargon Higgs凝聚不超导，预言C2奇(F3)电荷序、环流以及h/4e或h/6e微量配对相等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掺杂量子自旋液体通常被期待产生超导，尤其kagome体系；本文要解释为何kagome掺杂后先出现电荷序，并把掺杂Mott绝缘体与kagome金属纳入统一分类。

Method: 采用U(1) Dirac自旋液体的chargon(Higgs)构造，分析两个规范分量在线图效应下的劈裂；用投影对称群(PSG)把四阶竞争约化为一个角度，并用O(4)×U(1)重整化保护；同时建立电荷扇区的分类，作为未掺杂bilinear-and-monopole目录的带电对应。

Result: 一个规范分量色散，在-(1+√6)t处有四个极小；另一个有两条严格平的最低能带，位于-2t。仅色散分量在裸质量窗口(√6−1)t内凝聚，形成M、K和K/2星上的电荷与环流晶体，物理U(1)未破缺，即不超导的chargon Higgs相。配对通道属于Z2后代，为pair-density wave；M星上均匀d+id在领头阶及所有阶被排除。PSG下投影在位排斥恰好位于M与K/2边界，方向由O(4)×U(1)在所有阶受保护，但晶格匹配给出偏离射线的物理耦合。竞争M星晶体直到六阶仍简并，八阶才分裂。预言包括C2奇(F3)电荷序、无立方不变量、周期h/4e或h/6e的vestigial pair phases、herbertsmithite中17O与35Cl约十高斯的环流场，以及若Γ通量随凝聚序则电荷与电流序在凝聚尺度以下同时出现。

Conclusion: kagome掺杂自旋液体的先发电荷序由能带/线图效应驱动，形成不超导的chargon Higgs相；其电荷扇区分类和F-irrep网格统一了掺杂Mott绝缘体与kagome金属，并给出可检验的电荷序、环流和微量配对预言。

Abstract: Doping a quantum spin liquid is expected to give a superconductor. On kagome it gives charge order first, because of band structure rather than interaction. In the chargon (Higgs) construction of its U(1) Dirac spin liquid the two gauge components, degenerate on square and triangular lattices, are split by a line-graph effect: one disperses, four minima at $-(1+\sqrt6)t$; the other has two exactly flat lowest bands at $-2t$. Only the dispersive component condenses, over a bare-mass window of width exactly $(\sqrt6-1)t$, in which the doped spin liquid forms charge and loop-current crystals on the $M$, $K$ and $K/2$ stars, the physical U(1) unbroken: a chargon Higgs phase that does not superconduct. Its pairing channels are those of the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ descendants, a pair-density wave, uniform $d+id$ excluded at leading order and at all orders on the $M$ star. The projective symmetry group reduces the quartic competition to one angle, on which projected on-site repulsion sits exactly at the $M$/$K/2$ boundary, a direction protected to all orders by $O(4)\times U(1)$ under renormalization, though lattice matching gives the physical coupling off-ray components. The competing $M$-star crystals are degenerate through sextic order, split only at eighth. The charge sector carries its own classification, the charged counterpart of the undoped bilinear-and-monopole catalog, which holds no charge-carrying operator; it puts the doped Mott insulator and kagome metals on one $F$-irrep grid, separated by the charge-order irrep ($F_1$ there, $F_3$ here) and the Landau cubic. Predicted: $C_2$-odd ($F_3$) charge order, no cubic invariant; vestigial pair phases of period $h/4e$ or $h/6e$; loop-current fields of order ten gauss at herbertsmithite's $^{17}$O and $^{35}$Cl; and, if the $\Gamma$ flux orders with the condensate, charge and current order setting in together below the condensation scale.

</details>


### [128] [Vestigial altermagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14228)
*Peng Rao,Johannes Knolle*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在方格子多序竞争体系中，反铁磁（AFM）与轨道序（OO）热熔化可产生波矢 Q=0 的 vestigial altermagnetism（AM），其相变级次依赖裸磁化率和相互作用，并可通过 AM 对称的自能修正影响电子谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多竞争相在升温时，单个序虽被破坏，涨落仍可由序参量乘积形成 vestigial 序；本文旨在揭示 AFM 与 OO 熔化如何产生并刻画 vestigial AM 的相图与实验特征。

Method: 在方格子考虑 AFM 与 OO 共存，分析其热熔化与 vestigial AM 序参量；研究相变级次对裸磁化率和相互作用的依赖，并用最小两轨道模型计算电子谱函数和自能修正。

Result: 发现除 Q=(π,π) 常规 AM 外，AFM 与 OO 热熔化可产生 Q=0 的 vestigial AM；从高温无序态进入该相的转变可为一阶或二阶，取决于 AFM/OO 裸磁化率及相互作用。电子谱中，尽管 vestigial AM 序参量不直接耦合电子，涨落主导的自能给出自旋和轨道依赖的准粒子寿命，并具有 AM 对称性。

Conclusion: AFM 与 OO 的热熔化可稳定一种独特的 Q=0 vestigial AM 相；其相变性质与相互作用相关，且可通过准粒子寿命的 AM 对称各向异性作为实验探针。

Abstract: Interaction effects and fluctuations govern the phase diagram of systems with multiple competing phases. In particular, as individual phases are destroyed by increasing temperature, fluctuations can still produce a composite `vestigial' order given by a product of individual order parameters. Here, we describe vestigial Altermagnetism (AM) from the thermal melting of antiferromagnetic (AFM) and orbital orders (OO) on the square lattice. The coexistence of AFM and OO can give rise to conventional AM with ordering wave vector $\mathbf{Q}=(\pi,\pi)$. We show that a distinct `vestigial AM' phase with $\mathbf{Q}=0$ can arise from thermal melting of AFM and OO, and investigate its generic phase diagram. We find that the transition from the high-temperature disordered phase into the vestigial AM phase can be either first or second order, depending on the AFM and OO bare susceptibilities and their interactions. We then investigate the unique experimental signatures by calculating the electron spectral function for a minimal two-orbital model. Despite the fact that the vestigial AM order parameter does not couple to electrons directly, fluctuations dominate the self-energy corrections giving rise to a spin- and orbital-dependent quasi-particle lifetime with the AM symmetry.

</details>


### [129] [Reservoir-conditioned virtual returns generate random Liouvillian skin localization in a reciprocal Mott insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14390)
*Y. T. Wang,X. Z. Zhang,W. M. Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在半满Hubbard链中，通过粒子数守恒且方向选择性地返回虚doublon-hole缺陷，可将电荷运动与自旋相干分离，得到非对称自旋交换；平衡随机返回引发随机Liouvillian趋肤局域化，并决定精确硬核稳态与随机势垒控制的慢弛豫，从而在不改变互易Hamiltonian的情况下调控受限开放量子物质的输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方向性耗散能使弛豫模式在空间上聚集，但在关联物质中，要把这种聚集与可控微观过程联系起来，必须区分虚电荷运动与自旋相干。

Method: 研究半满Hubbard链，其中 hopping 互易，并加入粒子数守恒、方向选择的虚doublon-hole缺陷返回。先消除电荷缺陷，再消除Mott相干，在明确时间尺度分离下推导有效自旋动力学，并用短链完整动力学验证该约化。进一步分析平衡随机返回及累积速率失衡。

Result: 约化得到非对称自旋交换率。平衡随机返回使稳态布居和若干低能右本征模式聚集在样本选定位置，而非预定边界，产生随机Liouvillian趋肤局域化，尽管端到端对数速率偏置为零。累积速率失衡给出有限密度硬核的精确稳态，以及由随机势垒控制的一粒子激活弛豫尺度。

Conclusion: 局域校准的返回率、多体稳态权重和慢弛豫之间的联系，提供了一种在受限开放量子物质中控制输运的方法，且无需改变互易Hamiltonian。

Abstract: Directional dissipation can concentrate relaxation modes in space, but connecting this accumulation to controlled microscopic processes in correlated matter requires separating virtual charge motion from spin coherence. We study this connection in a half-filled Hubbard chain with reciprocal hopping and number-conserving, direction-selective returns of virtual doublon--hole defects. Eliminating charge defects and then Mott coherences yields asymmetric spin-exchange rates under an explicit separation of timescales, with full short-chain dynamics supporting the reduction on finite exchange times. Balanced random returns concentrate the stationary population and several low-lying right modes at sample-selected positions rather than a predetermined edge, producing random Liouvillian skin localization despite zero end-to-end logarithmic rate bias. The accumulated rate imbalance determines an exact finite-density hard-core stationary state and a one-particle activated relaxation scale governed by random barriers. This connection between locally calibrated return rates, many-body stationary weights and slow relaxation offers a means of controlling transport in constrained open quantum matter without changing the reciprocal Hamiltonian.

</details>


### [130] [Kagome lattice candidate URhSn: Measured Bragg diffraction patterns and theory for hexagonal and trigonal Sohncke phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14429)
*S. W. Lovesey,D. D. Khalyavin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: URhSn 的中间相（16–54 K）被共振 X 射线衍射和对称性分析支持为三方 Sohncke（手性）结构，其序参量为含十六极的铁性序参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ZrNiAl 型三元金属间化合物中的 kagome 几何阻挫导致复杂相图；5f 化合物 URhSn 在 16 K 和 54 K 发生两次相变，但中子粉末衍射在中间温区未见超晶格反射，近期共振 XRD 又提示手性三方 Sohncke 结构，需澄清其对称性与序参量。

Method: 采用对称性 informed 理论分析铀离子共振 X 射线衍射：用灰群修正原数据分析中的对称性，取消对铀位置的不合理空间约束；基于满足 X 射线二色性求和规则的轴向原子多极描述，计算并与观测衍射图样比较。

Result: 理论表明中间相可用含十六极的铁性序参量表征，并支持三方 Sohncke 相；计算与观测共振 XRD 图样相符，同时暴露出需要进一步实验验证的问题。

Conclusion: URhSn 中间相很可能为三方 Sohncke 手性相，具有十六极铁性序；该对称性理论修正了先前分析并指出对该神秘化合物仍需更多实验研究。

Abstract: Ternary intermetallics use a ZrNiAl-type chemical structure known as a kagome system. Complexity observed in the phase diagrams of these compounds is attributed to the geometric frustration. The 5f-electron compound URhSn exhibits two phase transitions at 16 K and 54 K. No super-lattice reflections in neutron powder diffraction patterns are detected in the intermediate range of temperatures (16 K - 54 K). Recent resonant x-ray diffraction patterns for uranium ions in the intermediate phase suggest a chiral structure and a trigonal Sohncke space group. It is firmly established that a simple hexagonal space group describes the parent structure at room temperature. A ferroic order parameter that includes hexadecapoles is shown to characterize the trigonal Sohncke phase. Resonant x-ray diffraction by this phase is fully investigated with our symmetry informed theory. It repairs symmetry in the original data analysis by using a grey group, and it lifts an unjustified spatial condition on uranium positions. The theory uses axial atomic multipoles that satisfy sum-rules established in the first instance for x-ray dichroic signals. A confrontation between observed and calculated diffraction patterns lends support to a trigonal Sohncke phase, and exposes grounds for additional experimental work on an enigmatic compound.

</details>


### [131] [Finite-temperature quantum Monte Carlo study of Dirac quantum criticality: Algorithmic advances and comprehensive analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14668)
*Hou-Min Du,Qian Sun,Yuan-Yao He*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 用有限温度 AFQMC 研究蜂窝晶格 Hubbard 模型中 Dirac 半金属到反铁磁 Mott 绝缘体的量子相变，确定临界点与临界指数，并为手征 Heisenberg Gross-Neveu-Yukawa 临界性提供独立基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统研究蜂窝 Hubbard 模型中的 Dirac 半金属—反铁磁 Mott 绝缘体量子相变，检验并基准手征 Heisenberg Gross-Neveu-Yukawa 临界性，补充以往基态模拟。

Method: 采用有限温度辅助场量子蒙特卡洛（AFQMC）；利用动力学临界指数 z=1，令 βt=L 同时逼近零温与热力学极限；将路径传播中的快速傅里叶变换推广到多子晶格系统并使用延迟更新加速；采用 twist-averaged 边界条件；通过自旋关联和非对角单粒子格林函数的有限尺寸标度确定临界性，并用准粒子权重、Fermi-liquid 参数、电荷压缩率、双占据的 U 导数和 fidelity susceptibility 佐证。模拟最大规模达 3528 个格点（L=42, βt=42）。

Result: 定位临界点 U_c/t=3.731(4)，得到临界指数 ν=1.097(7)、η_φ=0.72(2)、η_ψ=0.155(6)。

Conclusion: 该工作为蜂窝晶格 Hubbard 模型中的手征 Heisenberg Gross-Neveu-Yukawa 临界性建立了独立基准，并为先前基态模拟提供了互补视角。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of the quantum phase transition from a Dirac semimetal to an antiferromagnetic Mott insulator in the honeycomb-lattice Hubbard model, using finite-temperature auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo (AFQMC) simulations. Given the dynamical critical exponent $z=1$ at criticality, we scale the inverse temperature $\beta$ with the linear system size $L$, i.e., $\beta t=L$ (with $t$ as the hopping amplitude), to approach the thermodynamic and zero-temperature limits simultaneously. On the algorithmic front, we generalize the fast Fourier transform in path propagation to multi-sublattice systems and employ the delayed update technique in configuration updates to accelerate the AFQMC simulations. These advances enable us to access unprecedented system sizes of up to $3528$ sites (with $L=42$ and $\beta t = 42$). We additionally use twist-averaged boundary conditions to reduce the finite-size effects in computed observables. On the physical side, we determine the Dirac quantum criticality via finite-size scaling analysis of the spin correlations and off-diagonal single-particle Green's function, and locate the critical point at $U_c/t=3.731(4)$ with the critical exponents $\nu=1.097(7)$, $\eta_{\phi}=0.72(2)$ and $\eta_{\psi}=0.155(6)$. We further corroborate the critical point by examining several relevant quantities, including the quasiparticle weight, Fermi-liquid parameter, charge compressibility, $U$-derivative of double occupancy, and fidelity susceptibility. Our work establishes an independent benchmark for the chiral Heisenberg Gross-Neveu-Yukawa criticality in the honeycomb Hubbard model, offering a complementary perspective to previous ground-state simulations.

</details>


### [132] [Rhombohedral Multilayer Graphene as a $p$-Wave Magnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14705)
*Rintaro Eto,Johannes Knolle*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文提出在面内磁场下的菱方多层石墨烯可作为高度可调的非相对论共线p波磁性平台，无需自旋轨道耦合即可产生奇宇称自旋劈裂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找不依赖自旋轨道耦合、可通过外场调控的p波磁性或奇宇称自旋劈裂机制，并利用菱方多层石墨烯的轨道耦合与层间反铁磁关联实现。

Method: 采用最小低能有效理论分析面内磁场轨道耦合与相互作用驱动层反铁磁性的共同作用；并用更现实的Hubbard模型估算空穴掺杂下的稳定性及输运特征。

Result: 面内磁场破坏时间反演对称性，与层反铁磁序结合产生动量奇函数自旋劈裂；该p波自旋劈裂随层数增加而显著增强，源于低能态的表面局域性和层依赖的轨道耦合；Zeeman耦合仅弱扰动该轨道场主导机制。

Conclusion: 外磁场的轨道耦合为共线磁体中的奇宇称自旋劈裂提供了一条可控途径，并确立了菱方多层石墨烯作为研究非相对论p波磁性的可调平台。

Abstract: We propose rhombohedral multilayer graphene in an applied in-plane magnetic field as a highly tunable platform for nonrelativistic collinear $p$-wave magnetism. The orbital coupling to the magnetic field breaks time-reversal symmetry and, together with interaction-driven layer antiferromagnetism, generates an odd-in-momentum spin splitting without relying on spin-orbit coupling. Using a minimal low-energy effective theory, we show that the resulting $p$-wave spin splitting is strongly enhanced with increasing layer number. This enhancement originates from the surface-localized nature of the low-energy states and their layer-dependent orbital coupling to the in-plane field. The Zeeman coupling only weakly perturbs this predominantly orbital-field-induced mechanism. We further estimate stability of the $p$-wave magnetism under hole-doping and distinct transport signature using a more realistic Hubbard model. Our results establish orbital coupling to an external magnetic field as a controllable route to odd-parity spin splitting in collinear magnets.

</details>


### [133] [The Propagating Nature of Single- and Multimagnons in LaCrO$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14792)
*Masoud Lazemi,Fabian J. Mohammad,Ellen M. Kiens,Yorick A. Birkhölzer,Paras Mehta,Emma van der Minne,Stefano Agrestini,Frank M.F. de Groot,Hebatalla Elnaggar*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用动量与温度依赖的RIXS研究LaCrO3中的多磁振子激发，揭示单磁振子强色散而双/三磁振子近乎无色散，并通过热激发的反斯托克斯支证明其传播性组分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上以色散判断激发是否传播、以无色散判断是否局域，但复合激发即使由传播准粒子构成，其谱峰也可能近乎无色散，因此需要阐明多磁振子激发的微观起源。

Method: 采用动量分辨和温度依赖的共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS），并结合热加权多磁振子联合态密度（JDOS）计算进行分析。

Result: 在反铁磁LaCrO3中，单磁振子强色散，而二磁振子和三磁振子特征几乎平坦；接近Néel温度时，磁振子带的热布居产生反斯托克斯支，并开启产生两个磁振子、湮灭一个磁振子的额外三磁振子通道，其能量和动量依赖可由热加权多磁振子JDOS重现。

Conclusion: 热布居可以暴露隐藏于无色散复合激发中的传播组分，并使RIXS成为研究多磁振子动力学乃至磁振子-磁振子相互作用的有效探针。

Abstract: The dispersion of an excitation is commonly used to infer its microscopic character: dispersive modes are associated with propagation, whereas nondispersive features are often interpreted as localized. This distinction becomes less straightforward for composite excitations, whose spectral maxima can remain nearly dispersionless even when they are built from propagating quasiparticles. Here we use momentum- and temperature-dependent resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS) to reveal the origin of multimagnon excitations in antiferromagnetic LaCrO$_3$. Whereas the single magnon disperses strongly, the two- and three-magnon features remain nearly flat. On approaching the Neel temperature, thermal population of the magnon band produces an anti-Stokes branch and opens an additional three-magnon channel involving the creation of two magnons and annihilation of one. Its energy and momentum dependence are reproduced by a thermally weighted multimagnon joint density of states calculations. These results establish thermal population as a means of exposing the propagating constituents hidden within nondispersive composite excitations, and position RIXS as a probe of multimagnon dynamics and, ultimately, magnon-magnon interactions.

</details>


### [134] [Quantum Hall Ferromagnetism in a Cavity Vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14838)
*Ceren B. Dag,Luis Brey,Ganpathy Murthy,H.A. Fertig*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 腔真空涨落可在填充因子 ν=1 的量子霍尔铁磁体中驱动连续相变，形成由致密双占据核心与均匀量子霍尔铁磁侧翼组成的“致密核相”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索腔真空涨落能否单独稳定电子相，并在量子霍尔铁磁体中寻找由光-物质耦合驱动的连续相变及可实验探测的序参量。

Method: 在偶极规范下进行朗道能级投影，将态近似为电子与光子自由度的张量积；结合自旋波解析计算、平均场理论和 DMRG，并解析推导乘积电子态的相变边界，研究薄柱极限及纠缠谱简并。

Result: 空间反对称腔场下，弱耦合时均匀量子霍尔铁磁态稳定；超过临界耦合后出现电子密度不均匀的致密核相，其核心为双占据轨道、核心尺寸为序参量。全自旋极化态为乘积态，而 Sz=0 磁扇区中均匀侧翼间通过核心产生纠缠。相变边界对乘积电子态可精确导出并经 DMRG 验证；光子数可探测该序参量，提供实验签名。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一个罕见的、仅由腔模增强真空涨落耦合所稳定的电子相，并表明光子数可作为电子相变与致密核态的实验探针。

Abstract: We uncover a continuous phase transition in a quantum Hall ferromagnet (QHF) at filling factor $\nu=1$, driven by vacuum fluctuations of a cavity. Our analysis starts with a Landau level projection in dipole gauge, where we find the states to be well-represented by a tensor product of the electronic and photonic degrees of freedom. Through analytic spin wave calculations, mean-field theory and density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) simulations, we show that for a spatially antisymmetric cavity field, the uniform QHF state is stable only for weak light-matter coupling and gives way to states of inhomogeneous electron density above a critical coupling. These states involve "flanks" of uniform QHF fluids, separated by a "compact core" of doubly occupied orbitals with the core size being the order parameter, which we dub as "compact-core phases". While the fully spin polarized electronic states are product states, entanglement builds up between the uniform QHF flanks across the compact core in the $S_z=0$ magnetic sector, motivating an ansatz for the compact-core electronic states. The transition boundary is exactly derived for product electronic states in terms of matter and cavity parameters, and numerically confirmed by DMRG. We also study the thin-cylinder limit near the critical point where quantum fluctuations are enhanced, and the many-body excited states in both phases by focusing on the entanglement spectrum degeneracies. Remarkably, the photon number is found to probe the order parameter of the transition, providing a possible experimental signature of the electronic transition and the compact-core states. Our study offers a rare example of a phase of electrons stabilized solely by coupling to the enhanced vacuum fluctuations of a cavity mode.

</details>


### [135] [Heisenberg-like critical behavior in the quasi-two-dimensional metallic ferromagnet LaCrSb3](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14994)
*Qianhui Mao,Haotian Deng,Bin Chen,Jinhu Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过单晶等温磁化与临界行为分析，确定LaCrSb3铁磁相变TC=126 K及临界指数，证明其磁性相变基本属于三维Heisenberg普适类，并指出二维极限下可能具有有限温度磁序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LaCrSb3同时表现出准二维自旋涨落和三维磁相互作用特征，其铁磁相变的临界性质及本征磁相互作用机制尚不明确。

Method: 测量单晶在临界点附近的高精度等温磁化曲线；基于Arrott-Noakes方程采用自洽迭代法提取临界指数；利用Widom标度律、磁状态标度方程等验证可靠性；与理论模型比较判定普适类，并通过交换相互作用J(r)的距离依赖衰减行为分析主导相互作用。

Result: 确定铁磁-顺磁相变居里温度TC=126 K，临界指数β=0.376、γ=1.417、δ=4.76；临界指数经标度关系验证可靠；体系临界行为基本属于三维Heisenberg模型普适类，且各向同性直接交换相互作用占主导。

Conclusion: LaCrSb3的磁相变具有三维Heisenberg临界特征，同时结合其他准二维磁性材料研究推测其在二维极限下可能呈现有限温度磁序，具有重要理论研究意义和潜在应用前景。

Abstract: LaCrSb3 is a material exhibiting both quasi-two-dimensional spin fluctuations and three-dimensional magnetic interaction characteristics. By measuring the isothermal magnetization of single-crystals and conducting a systematic critical behavior analysis, we clarify the critical properties of its ferromagnetic phase transition and the intrinsic magnetic interaction mechanism. Based on high-precision isothermal magnetization data measured in the vicinity of the critical point, the Curie temperature for the ferromagnetic-paramagnetic phase transition is determined to be TC = 126 K, with the critical exponents obtained as $\beta$ = 0.376, $\gamma$ = 1.417 and $\delta$ = 4.76 via the self-consistent iterative method based on the Arrott-Noakes equation. The reliability of these critical exponents is verified by the Widom scaling law, the magnetic state scaling equation and other analyses. A comparison with theoretical models demonstrates that the critical behavior of the magnetic phase transition in this system basically belongs to the universality class of the three-dimensional Heisenberg model. This conclusion is further confirmed by the distance-dependent decay behavior of the exchange interaction J(r), revealing the dominant role of isotropic direct exchange interactions in this system. Finally, drawing on research findings of other quasi-two-dimensional magnetic materials, this work proposes that LaCrSb3 may exhibit finite-temperature magnetic order in the two-dimensional limit, thereby possessing important theoretical research significance and promising practical application prospects.

</details>


### [136] [Observation of complex orbital order in MnV2O4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15060)
*Chihaya Koyama,Shunsuke Kitou,Taishun Manjo,Yuiga Nakamura,Takeshi Hara,Naoyuki Katayama,Takuro Katsufuji,Ryotaro Arita,Yusuke Nomura,Hiroshi Sawa,Taka-hisa Arima*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 结合高精度单晶同步辐射X射线衍射与核心微分傅里叶合成，在实空间可视化MnV2O4轨道有序基态的价电子密度，确定低温结构为I41/amd，并揭示不同于已有实/复轨道模型的轨道有序态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钒尖晶石氧化物中几何阻挫烧绿石结构的轨道有序长期存在争议，源于竞争性理论模型及直接实验证据缺乏。

Method: 采用高精度单晶同步辐射X射线衍射与核心微分傅里叶合成，在实空间重建价电子密度；仔细处理多重散射伪影以确定低温结构，并分析V位点周围VED。

Result: 低温结构被确定为I41/amd空间群；V位点周围重建的VED显示一种轨道有序态，既不同于此前提出的实轨道模型，也不同于复轨道模型。

Conclusion: 结果解决了MnV2O4中长期争议，并为识别阻挫自旋-轨道体系中的轨道态建立了途径。

Abstract: Orbital ordering in vanadium spinel oxides with a geometrically frustrated pyrochlore structure has been a subject of controversy, owing to competing theoretical models and the absence of direct experimental evidence. Here we combine high-precision single-crystal synchrotron x-ray diffraction with core differential fourier synthesis to visualize the valence electron density (VED) of the orbital-ordered ground state in real space. By carefully investigating multiple-scattering artifacts, we identify the low-temperature structure as belonging to the I41/amd space group. The reconstructed VED around the V sites reveals an orbital-ordered state distinct from both previously proposed real- and complex-orbital models. Our results resolve the long-standing controversy in MnV2O4 and establish a route to identifying orbital states in frustrated spin-orbital systems.

</details>


### [137] [Non-Abelian chiral spin liquid in a spin-$1$ antiferromagnet on the square lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15061)
*Xiao-Tian Zhang,Yuan Yang,D. N. Sheng,Shou-Shu Gong*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文在自旋-1方格子模型中发现了非阿贝尔手性自旋液体（CSL），并通过DMRG计算给出了量子相图及Moore-Read态的关键证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔CSL具有非阿贝尔任意子激发，是拓扑量子计算的重要平台，但在短程相互作用自旋系统中仍难以实现。

Method: 研究含第一、第二近邻双线性相互作用（J1,J2）、双二次相互作用（K1,K2）及三自旋标量手性耦合（Jχ）的自旋-1方格子模型；受ED和iPEPS中Moore-Read态证据启发，固定J1=1.0、J2/J1=0.623、K1/J1=-0.176，用DMRG计算0≤K2/J1≤0.4和0.3≤Jχ/J1≤0.6范围内的量子相图。

Result: 识别出条纹反铁磁相、Néel反铁磁相、磁性有序手性自旋态相，以及出现在有序相边界附近的非阿贝尔CSL相；DMRG结果给出Moore-Read态证据，包括三个拓扑扇区、量子化自旋陈数C=1，以及低能纠缠谱能级计数与手性SU(2)_2共形场论一致。

Conclusion: 该结果为在更高自旋（S>1/2）且具有短程耦合的系统中寻找非阿贝尔CSL提供了新线索。

Abstract: Non-Abelian chiral spin liquids (CSLs) host non-Abelian anyon excitations and are promising platforms for topological quantum computation. However, realizing non-Abelian CSLs in short-range interacting spin systems remains elusive. Here, we study a spin-$1$ square-lattice model with the first- and second-neighbor bilinear ($J_1,J_2$) and biquadratic interactions ($K_1,K_2$), as well as the three-spin scalar chiral coupling $J_{\chi}$. Inspired by the evidence of the Moore-Read state in exact diagonalization and infinite Projected Entangled Pair States calculations, we fix $J_1=1.0$, $J_2/J_1=0.623$, $K_1/J_1=-0.176$, and obtain a quantum phase diagram for $0 \leq K_2/J_1 \leq 0.4$ and $0.3 \leq J_{\chi}/J_1 \leq 0.6$ by using the density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations. We identify a stripe antiferromagnetic phase, a N\'eel antiferromagnetic phase, a magnetically ordered chiral spin state phase, and a non-Abelian CSL phase emerging near the boundaries of the ordered phases. The unbiased DMRG results provide strong evidence for the Moore-Read state, including the three topological sectors, the quantized spin Chern number $C=1$, and the level counting of low-lying entanglement spectra which is consistent with the chiral SU(2)$_2$ conformal field theory. Our results may shed new light on searching for non-Abelian CSLs in other higher-spin ($S > 1/2$) systems with short-range couplings.

</details>


### [138] [Band-Saddle-Point Engineering in Mixed $A$-site $A$V$_3$Sb$_5$ Kagome Metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15196)
*Maxim Wenzel,Alexander A. Tsirlin,Andrea N. Capa Salinas,Brenden R. Ortiz,Stephen D. Wilson,Ece Uykur,Martin Dressel*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用混合A位AV3Sb5的光学光谱结合DFT，发现A位碱金属通过Cs替代诱导能带鞍点反转，调控范霍夫奇点的轨道特征、费米面拓扑和电子-声子相互作用，同时不破坏kagome层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AV3Sb5 (A=K, Rb, Cs) 是研究接近理想范霍夫填充的kagome金属物理的平台，但A位碱金属如何影响低能电子结构尚需阐明。

Method: 对具有混合A位组分的AV3Sb5进行光学光谱测量，并用密度泛函理论计算提供支持。

Result: 识别出能带鞍点反转是Cs替代的化学结果；A位组分控制范霍夫奇点的轨道特征、费米面拓扑和电子-声子相互作用，而kagome层保持完整。

Conclusion: A位化学组分是调控AV3Sb5低能电子结构和范霍夫奇点物理的有效自由度，可在不破坏kagome层的前提下改变其电子结构与电子-声子耦合行为。

Abstract: $A$V$_3$Sb$_5$ compounds ($A$ = K, Rb, Cs) offer an experimental platform for probing the physics of kagome metals near ideal Van Hove filling. Here, we use optical spectroscopy on $A$V$_3$Sb$_5$ with mixed $A$-site compositions to reveal the effect of the alkali metal on the low-energy electronic structure. Supported by density-functional-theory calculations, we identify a band-saddle-point inversion as a chemical consequence of Cs substitution, revealing that $A$-site composition controls the orbital character of Van Hove singularities, Fermi surface topology, and electron-phonon interactions while leaving the kagome layer intact.

</details>


### [139] [Twinning of domains and spin anisotropy in K$_5$Fe$_4$Ag$_6$Te$_{10}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15290)
*Jiayu Guo,Hengyang Zhong,Dongsheng Yuan,Xuejuan Gui,Youzhe Chen,Nathan Giles-Donovan,Naomi Kawamura,Masaaki Matsuda,Yaohua Liu,Feng Ye,Rongyan Chen,Robert J. Birgeneau,Xingye Lu,Jincheng Wang,Yu Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 利用飞行时间单晶中子衍射全倒空间测量，解析了 KFAT 的 √5×√5 超结构及四个等布居畴（两个超结构畴和两个磁畴），并用极化中子散射揭示 q≠0 的显著自旋各向异性与易磁化面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KFAT 是 Fe 基半导体，具有与 Fe 基超导体类似的向列序和条纹磁序，可作为局域电子极限下研究 Fe 基超导体磁性、向列序及畴结构的类比体系。

Method: 采用飞行时间单晶中子衍射全面映射倒空间，结合弹性中子散射和极化中子散射，解析超结构、磁畴/向列畴及自旋各向异性。

Result: Fe 与 Ag 有序形成包含 2×2 Fe 块的 √5×√5 超结构，并产生两个主布拉格峰相同但超结构峰不同的超结构畴；T_N≈35 K 以下，磁序和向列序破坏四方 √5×√5 超结构的面内旋转对称性，进一步形成两个磁畴，共四个等布居畴可解释复杂散射图样；极化中子散射显示 c 轴与块内反铁磁 Fe-Fe 键方向张成的易磁化面具有显著自旋各向异性，且该 q≠0 各向异性在 T_N 以上仍持续存在。

Conclusion: 该 q≠0 自旋各向异性可解释单轴应变 KFAT 中观察到的 q=0 面内磁各向异性，并为在其他材料中寻找类似压磁效应提供指示。

Abstract: The Fe-based superconductors are derived from metallic parent compounds with nematic and stripe magnetic orders, which lead to two types of magnetic domains. Recently it was found that K$_5$Fe$_4$Ag$_6$Te$_{10}$ (KFAT), an Fe-based semiconductor, exhibits similar nematic and stripe magnetic orders, and is thus an analogue to the Fe-based superconductors in the limit of localized electrons. In this work, the superstructure and magnetic domains of KFAT are elucidated by fully mapping the reciprocal space using time-of-flight single crystal neutron diffraction. In KFAT, Fe and Ag atoms order to form a $\sqrt{5}\times\sqrt{5}$ superstructure containing $2\times2$ Fe blocks, which leads to two superstructure domains with identical main Bragg peaks but distinct superstructure peaks. Below $T_{\rm N}\approx35$~K, magnetic and nematic orders break in-plane rotational symmetry of the tetragonal $\sqrt{5}\times\sqrt{5}$ superstructure, and further give rise to two magnetic domains. These four equally populated domains account for the complex scattering pattern observed in our time-of-flight elastic neutron scattering measurements. Using polarized neutron scattering, we demonstrate a prominent spin anisotropy with an easy-plane spanned by the $c$-axis and the intra-block antiferromagnetic Fe-Fe bond direction. Such an anisotropy at ${\bf q}\neq0$ persists well above $T_{\rm N}$, accounts for the in-plane ${\bf q}=0$ magnetic anisotropy observed in uniaxial-strained KFAT, and offers an indicator for discovering similar piezomagnetic effects in other materials.

</details>


### [140] [Engineering Competing Fractional Topological States in a BHZ Superlattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15300)
*Ke-Chen Liu,Chen Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在 QWZ 陈绝缘体中引入标量超晶格可重构出孤立的 C=+1 微带并实现 1/3 分数量子陈绝缘体；将其时间反演伙伴恢复为 BHZ 超晶格后，在总填充 2/3 下出现平衡分数量子自旋霍尔态与全极化分数陈绝缘体的竞争，并可由层间耦合和赝自旋各向异性调控选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量超晶格为从简单拓扑带走向分数化态提供了途径，而多分量拓扑体系还需回答哪种分数序会在能量上被选中，即分数态形成之后的分数态选择问题。

Method: 对 Qi-Wu-Zhang（QWZ）Chern 绝缘体模型施加标量超晶格，得到孤立 C=+1 微带；对投影相互作用进行精确对角化以识别分数陈绝缘体。再恢复时间反演伙伴构成 Bernevig-Hughes-Zhang（BHZ）超晶格，在总填充 2/3 下比较平衡 FQSH-like 态与全极化 2/3 分数陈绝缘体，并分析有限动量分量间耦合和均匀赝自旋各向异性的影响。

Result: QWZ 超晶格产生的 C=+1 微带具有显著改善的 Berry 曲率与量子度规均匀性，投影相互作用精确对角化给出 1/3 分数陈绝缘体。BHZ 超晶格在总填充 2/3 下同时存在平衡 FQSH-like 态和全极化 2/3 分数陈绝缘体；有限动量分量间耦合决定平衡 FQSH-like 态的稳定性，均匀赝自旋各向异性则移动守恒扇区的相对能量，可将全局基态在平衡态与极化态之间切换。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个最小晶格平台，将拓扑微带重构、分数态形成与分数态选择联系起来，说明可以组合不同层次的控制来导航相互竞争的分数量子拓扑态。

Abstract: Scalar superlattices offer a route from simple topological bands to fractionalized states, while multicomponent systems raise the additional question of which fractional order is energetically selected. We show that a scalar superlattice applied to the Qi-Wu-Zhang (QWZ) Chern-insulator model produces an isolated C = +1 miniband with strongly improved Berry-curvature and quantum metric uniformity. Exact diagonalization of the projected interaction identifies a 1/3 fractional Chern insulator in this miniband. Restoring the time-reversed partner to form the Bernevig-Hughes-Zhang (BHZ) superlattice yields, at total filling 2/3, a balanced fractional quantum spin Hall (FQSH)-like state and a fully polarized 2/3 fractional Chern insulator. Their competition separates two aspects of fractional-state control: the finite-momentum intercomponent coupling governs the stability of the balanced FQSH-like state, whereas a uniform pseudospin anisotropy shifts the relative energies of conserved sectors and can switch the global ground state between the balanced and polarized states. These results establish a minimal lattice setting that connects topological-miniband reconstruction, fractional-state formation, and fractional-state selection, illustrating how distinct levels of control can be combined to navigate competing fractional topological states.

</details>


### [141] [Hidden Magnetic Complexity Within a Simple van der Waals Ferromagnet Ce$_2$Te$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15815)
*Rajesh Tripathi,D. T. Adroja,C. Ritter,Leandro Liborio,Manuel dos Santos Dias,Leon Petit,D. Khalyavin,Yu Liu,T. Shiroka,M. Aouane,S. Langridge,S. Patil,Eric D. Bauer*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: Ce2Te5 在 TC=5.0 K 发生单一长程磁有序，但低温 μSR 异常来自竞争相互作用与晶体电场效应对有序态的细微调制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究层状 f 电子范德华磁体 Ce2Te5 的磁基态，因其维度降低和两种晶体学不等价 Ce 位点导致竞争磁相互作用。

Method: 结合零场 μ 子自旋弛豫（μSR）、中子粉末衍射（NPD）和非弹性中子散射（INS）进行表征。

Result: 零场 μSR 显示 TC=5.0(1) K 以下出现静态磁性，并在 T2=2.3(2) K 出现内场异常，与体热力学和输运测量特征一致；NPD 在 0.05–8 K 仅观测到 TC 以下单一长程有序磁相，磁布拉格强度在 TC 消失，直至基温无额外结构或磁相变。有序态具有公度传播矢量 k=(0,0,0)，Ce 磁矩沿晶体 b 轴铁磁排列；仅两种晶体学不等价 Ce 位点中的一种携带约 0.85(3) μB/Ce 的有序磁矩（0.05 K）。INS 确立 Ce3+ 的晶体电场能标，发现 7.94 和 21.46 meV 低能激发，以及具强单离子各向异性的 Kramers 双重态基态。

Conclusion: Ce2Te5 经历单一对称破缺磁相变；额外的低温异常反映了竞争相互作用和晶体电场效应驱动下有序态的细微变化。

Abstract: Ce$_2$Te$_5$ is a layered $f$-electron van der Waals magnet in which reduced dimensionality and inequivalent Ce sites give rise to competing magnetic interactions. We investigate its magnetic ground state using muon spin relaxation ($\mu$SR), neutron powder diffraction (NPD), and inelastic neutron scattering (INS). Zero-field $\mu$SR reveals an onset of static magnetism below $T_{\mathrm{C}} = 5.0(1)$~K, followed by an additional anomaly in the internal field at $T_{\mathrm{2}} = 2.3(2)$~K, consistent with features observed in bulk thermodynamic and transport measurements. In contrast, NPD data collected between $0.05-8$~K reveal a single long-range ordered magnetic phase below $T_{\mathrm C}$, with the magnetic Bragg intensities vanishing at $T_{\mathrm C}$ with no evidence for additional structural or magnetic phase transitions down to base temperature. The ordered state is characterized by a commensurate propagation vector $\mathbf{k}=(0,0,0)$ and ferromagnetic alignment of Ce moments along the crystallographic $b$ axis. Remarkably, only one of the two crystallographically distinct Ce sites carries an ordered moment of $\sim 0.85(3)~\mu_{\mathrm B}$ per Ce at 0.05~K. INS measurements establish the crystal electric field (CEF) energy scale of Ce$^{3+}$, revealing low-lying excitations at 7.94 and 21.46~meV and a Kramers doublet ground state with strong single-ion anisotropy. These results demonstrate that Ce$_2$Te$_5$ undergoes a single symmetry-breaking magnetic transition, while additional low-temperature anomalies reflect subtle modifications of the ordered state driven by competing interactions and CEF effects.

</details>


### [142] [Chiral Antiferromagnetism from Momentum-Space Resonance in a 2D Semiconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15854)
*R. Okuma,T. Ikenobe,Y. Fujisawa,K. Yamagami,H. C. H. Wu,T. Nakamura,Y. Ihara,H. Ishikawa,H. Suwa,H. Ishizuka,Y. Akagi,T. Kaneko,C. H. Hsu,Y. Obata,N. Tomoda,M. Dronova,K. Nagasawa,H. Saito,D. Ueta,H. Sagayama,J. Yamaura,M. Arita,K. Yogendra,S. Ideta,K. Kindo,T. Nakajima,S. J. Blundell,T. Kondo,K. Shimada,Y. Okamoto,Y. Okada*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出以简单半导体带边为出发点设计手性量子物相，并在范德华半导体GdGaI中发现自发形成的交织手性三-q反铁磁态，伴随电子-空穴带边重构、贝里曲率和自发反常霍尔效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解手性量子相涌现机制既是基础挑战，也关系到利用手性与拓扑实现巨大电子响应和输运现象。金属中费米面不稳定性虽可稳定复杂有序态，但其微观起源常被复杂电子结构遮蔽，缺乏通用微观设计原则。

Method: 采用基于半导体带极值简单性的互补策略，选取低能电子结构仅含简单电子谷和空穴谷的层状范德华半导体GdGaI，研究其电子-空穴带边在动量空间中的共振式相互作用与自发对称性破缺。

Result: 发现GdGaI中超越常规单-q基态的自发交织手性三-q反铁磁态，并伴随电子-空穴带边协同重构；该集体重构产生显著动量空间贝里曲率，导致在半导体特性和净磁化近零条件下仍出现明显自发反常霍尔效应。该手性态存在于原子级明确（约2a）、拓扑非平凡磁织构中，表明简单二维磁性半导体可在极小长度尺度上产生此类集体量子态。

Conclusion: 研究提出一种极简的手性量子物质设计理念：以简单半导体带边作为动量空间共振式相互作用的构建模块，为贝里曲率、拓扑输运及涌现量子相提供路径。

Abstract: Understanding the principles governing the emergence of chiral quantum phases is a fundamental challenge, not only for uncovering new mechanisms of quantum-state formation but also for realizing giant electronic responses and transport phenomena arising from chirality and topology. While Fermi-surface instabilities in metals can stabilize complex ordered states through multiple competing scattering channels, their microscopic origin is often obscured by the complexity of the underlying electronic structure, limiting the development of general microscopic design principles. Here, we introduce a complementary strategy based on the simplicity of semiconductor band extrema. Using the layered van der Waals semiconductor GdGaI, whose low-energy electronic structure consists of simple electron and hole valleys, we discover the spontaneous emergence of an intertwined chiral triple-$q$ antiferromagnetic state accompanied by a cooperative reconstruction of the electron-hole band edges, beyond the conventional expectation of a single-$q$ ground state. This collective reconstruction generates substantial momentum-space Berry curvature, giving rise to a pronounced spontaneous anomalous Hall effect despite the semiconducting character and negligible net magnetization. Remarkably, this chiral state is realized within an atomically well-defined ($\approx2a$), topologically nontrivial magnetic texture, showing that such collective quantum states can emerge at an exceptionally small length scale from a simple two-dimensional magnetic semiconductor. More broadly, our results introduce a remarkably simple design concept for chiral quantum matter: using simple semiconductor band extrema as building blocks for resonance-like interplay in momentum space, providing a route to Berry curvature, topological transport, and emergent quantum phases.

</details>


### [143] [Fractional vortices in a spin-isotropic spiral spin liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15941)
*Cecilie Glittum,Han Yan,Johannes Reuther*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文分类并刻画了自旋各向同性海森堡螺旋自旋液体中的经典拓扑缺陷，发现其具有 Z × Z2 分类；基本缺陷是同时携带自旋与动量空间分数 2π 缠绕的半涡旋，融合规则类似 Ising anyon，并在方格子螺旋自旋液体模拟中表现为稠密、成键并最终湮灭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 螺旋自旋液体的经典基态流形由连续环或面上的平面非公度自旋螺旋构成，亚广延简并抑制长程磁序并产生液体行为。尽管已有大量材料实现和理论研究，其低温自旋构型结构仍不清楚。

Method: 对自旋各向同性海森堡螺旋自旋液体中的经典拓扑缺陷进行分类与表征，并对一个方格子螺旋自旋液体进行大规模经典模拟。

Result: 揭示了一类丰富涡旋，涉及自旋方向、螺旋面法向和波矢取向的协同缠绕，得到 Z × Z2 分类。基本缺陷为半涡旋，在自旋空间和动量空间均携带分数 2π 缠绕，其融合规则类似于 Ising anyon。模拟显示这些涡旋在螺旋自旋液体区稠密存在，在有序化相变以下紧密成键，并最终融合到真空。

Conclusion: 该工作阐明了螺旋自旋液体低温拓扑缺陷的结构与命运，指出其中存在分数化半涡旋和类 Ising anyon 融合行为，并将其与 order-by-disorder 相变及缺陷湮灭联系起来。

Abstract: Spiral spin liquids are magnetic states whose classical ground-state manifold consists of planar incommensurate spin spirals with wave vectors lying on a continuous ring or surface in reciprocal space. The resulting subextensive degeneracy suppresses magnetic long-range order and gives rise to liquid-like behavior. Despite numerous material realizations and theoretical investigations, the structure of low-temperature spin configurations of spin-isotropic Heisenberg spiral spin liquids has remained poorly understood. Here, we classify and characterize the classical topological defects supported by these systems. We uncover a rich family of vortex types involving concerted windings of spin directions, spiral-plane normals, and wave-vector orientations, yielding a $\mathbb{Z} \times \mathbb{Z}_2$ classification. Remarkably, the elementary defects are half-vortices carrying fractional $2\pi$ windings in both spin and momentum space and obey fusion rules resembling to those of Ising anyons. Large-scale classical simulations of a square-lattice spiral spin liquid reveal that these vortices are dense in the spiral-spin-liquid regime, and bind tightly below an order-by-disorder phase transition, eventually fusing to vacuum.

</details>
